###### Art. 257A.- Shall be punished with imprisonment of four to eight years the persons described

in the preceding article who, abusing their positions, have acted fraudulently to obtain or grant related, connected or intercompany credits, violating express legal provisions regarding this type of operation. The same penalty shall apply to beneficiaries who have fraudulently intervened in the commission of this offense and to those who have lent their name for their own benefit or that of a third party. Note: Article added by Art. 20 of Law No. 26, published in Official Register 190 of May 13, 1999.

###### Art. 257-A.- Persons elected by popular vote, representatives or delegates

of the President of the Republic and other fiscal or municipal officials in State agencies, autonomous or semi-autonomous; officials, employees or public servants who in any way use for their own benefit or for third parties when this signifies profit or patrimonial increase, workers remunerated by the State or by public sector entities or public sector assets, shall be punished with a penalty of one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred ninety to nine hundred fifty-two dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article added by Law No. 6, published in Official Registry Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

Note: Article amended by Art. 61 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 231, 232

###### Art. 257-B.- With the same penalty shall be sanctioned the persons mentioned in the

previous article, who have economically taken advantage for their own benefit or for third parties, of studies, projects, reports, resolutions and other documents, classified as secret, reserved or of restricted circulation, that are or have been within their knowledge or under their dependence by reason of or on occasion of the position they hold or have held.

Note: Article added by Law No. 6, published in Official Registry Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

###### Art. 257-C.- The same penalty mentioned in the previous articles shall be imposed on

persons elected by popular vote, representatives or delegates and public officials, employees or servants who taking advantage of the popular representation or the position they hold have favored or did favor natural or juridical persons so that, contrary to express legal or regulatory provisions, they have granted them contracts or allowed the conduct of business with the State or with any other agency of the public sector.

Included in the same previous provision and subject to the same penalty are the directors, members or participants of the administrative bodies of the State or the public sector in general, who, with their vote, have cooperated in the commission of the crime referred to in the preceding paragraph.

Note: Article added by Law No. 6, published in Official Registry Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

###### Art. 257-D.- It is the jurisdiction of the Criminal Court to try the crime of embezzlement

typified in article 257 of the Penal Code, subject to the procedure indicated in the current Code of Criminal Procedure and not to that determined in Decree No. 618 published in Official Registry 105 of July 19, 1972, in what remained in force according to the provisions of Supreme Decree No. 888 published in Official Registry No. 631 of September 4, 1974, and subsequently repealed since the Code of Criminal Procedure published in Official Registry No. 511 of June 10, 1983 came into force.

Note: Article given by Resolution in Plenary of the Supreme Court of Justice, published in Official Registry 572 of November 26, 1986.

###### Art. 258.

Note: Article suppressed by Art. 396 of the Organic Law of Financial Administration and Control, No. 1429, published in Official Registry 337 of May 16 of

###### Art. 259.

Note: Article suppressed by Art. 396 of the Organic Law of Financial Administration and Control, No. 1429, published in Official Registry 337 of May 16 of

###### Art. 260.- They shall be punished with imprisonment of two to four years, if the case is not

included in Art. 257, public officials and employees or their agents who, in charge of the acquisition or purchase of articles and supplies for the administration, receive commissions or premiums, alter the prices in the articles, the payrolls, etc., and the sellers or suppliers who take part or lend themselves to these illicit combinations.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF EMBEZZLEMENT, 21-Aug-2007

###### Art. 261.- Officials who collect by themselves, when they should do so through

another person, the fines they impose; or who do not grant a receipt of payment; or do not leave record of the fine in the corresponding book, shall be punished with fifteen days to one year of imprisonment and quadruple the fine unduly collected.

Note: Supreme Decree No. 380, of May 27, 1966, published in Official Registry No. 46, of June two of the same year, grants popular action to report to the corresponding authority cases of direct collection of fines by officials who imposed them and sanctions, in addition, such procedure, with the cancellation of the position and loss of remuneration.

###### Art. 262.- They shall be punished with three to six years of minor incarceration, every

public employee and every person in charge of a public service, who has maliciously and fraudulently destroyed or suppressed documents, titles, programs, data, databases, information or any data message contained in an information system or electronic network, of which they were depositaries, in their capacity as such, or that have been entrusted to them without reason of their position.

Note: Article substituted by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 11

###### Art. 263.- When pieces or criminal processes or other

papers, records or documents contained in archives, offices or public deposits have been removed or destroyed, or delivered to a public depositary, in his capacity as such, the depositary guilty of negligence shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, REMOVAL OF PROCESSES, 06-Nov-1951

###### Art. 264.- Public employees or persons in charge of a public service who

have been guilty of concussion, ordering to collect, demanding or receiving what they knew was not due for rights, fees, contributions, rents or interests, salaries or gratuities, shall be punished with imprisonment of two months to four years. The penalty shall be imprisonment of two to six years, if the concussion has been committed with violence or threats.

This penalty shall be applicable to prelates, priests or other ecclesiastics who demand from the faithful, against their will, tithes, first fruits, parochial rights, or any other obligations that are not authorized by civil Law. The infractions provided in this article and in the three preceding ones shall be punished, in addition, with a fine of forty dollars of the United States of North America and with the restitution of quadruple what they have received.

These penalties shall also be applied to the agents or official descendants of public employees and of every person in charge of a public service, according to the distinctions established above.

Note: Penultimate paragraph amended by Law No. 105, published in Official Registry 365 of July 21, 1998.

Note: Article amended by Art. 62 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 233

LABOR CODE, Arts. 523

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF CONCUSSION, 16-May-1930

###### Art. 265.- A public employee who, openly or by means of a simulated act by

him, or through an intermediary, takes for himself, in whole or in part, property or assets in whose auction, lease, adjudication, embargo, sequestration, judicial partition, deposit or administration, he intervenes by reason of his position or office; or any of the referred persons who enters equally in any business or speculation for profit or personal interest, related to the same properties or assets, or concerning a matter in which he has equal official intervention, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve percent of the value of the property or the business and with correctional imprisonment of six months to three years and the enforcement of the sentence may not be suspended.

Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO IV), Arts. 1737

###### Art. 266.- Criminal judges, treasurers, administrators and other employees of

Customs and Border Protection, who engage in commerce themselves, within the district where they respectively perform their functions, whether openly or through simulated acts, shall be punished with the loss of what is apprehended from them pertaining to this illicit commerce.

The same penalty shall be imposed on the President of the Republic, Ministers of State, Governors, General Commanders, Military Zone Chiefs and Magistrates of the courts, if they engage in commerce.

The provision of this article does not include the sale of products from estates that are owned by the employees, or that they manage as lessees, usufructuaries or users, nor the products from their own industry branches in which their relatives or their agents are engaged.

Nor is this provision applicable to those who place their funds in bank shares or of any enterprise or company, provided they do not exercise any position in them nor have direct, administrative or economic intervention, nor to those who lend their capital at interest. Concordancias:

LEY DE COMPAÑIAS, Arts. 101

CODIGO DE COMERCIO, Arts. 7

###### Art. 267.- A Magistrate or Judge who, fraudulently and while the lawsuit, proceeding or

matter of which he has knowledge is pending, becomes a debtor of one of the parties, or makes himself guarantor for one of them, or contracts with them any pecuniary obligation, shall be punished with a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America and suspension for three years of citizenship rights.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 63 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO CIVIL, Arts. 856

###### Art. 268.

Nota: Artículo derogado por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de

21 de Julio de 1998 .

###### Art. 269.

Nota: Artículo derogado por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de

21 de Julio de 1998 .

###### Art. 270.- A public official, agent shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years

or commissioner of the Government, whatever his employment or rank, who has required or ordered, caused to require or order, the action or use of public force against the execution of a Law or executive decree, against the collection of a legally established tax, or against the execution of a decree, order or judicial sentence, or of any order emanating from authority.

###### Art. 271.- If the requirement or order has taken effect, the guilty party shall be punished with

three to six years of minor reclusion.

###### Art. 272.- If the orders or requirements have been the direct cause of other crimes that

must be punished with more severe penalties than those expressed in the preceding articles, these more severe penalties shall be applied to the officials, agents or commissioners guilty of having given said orders or made said requirements.

###### Art. 273.- When a public official, of whatever nature, an agent of the

Government or Police, an executor of judicial orders or sentences, a Commander of the Public Force have, without legitimate motive, used or caused to use violence against any person, in the exercise or on occasion of the exercise of their functions, the minimum penalty established for these acts shall be increased in accordance with Art.

###### 275. Art. 274.- Any commander, officer or subordinate of the Public Force who, after

having been legally required by civil authority, has refused to provide the assistance requested by the latter, shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to three months. Concordancias:

CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 159

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 269

###### Art. 275.- Outside the case in which the Law specifically establishes the penalties with which

crimes committed by public officials or employees must be punished, those who have made themselves guilty of other crimes that they were charged with preventing, pursuing or punishing shall be sanctioned with the same penalties established for these crimes, doubling the minimum if the penalty is imprisonment, and increasing by two years if it is major or minor reclusion.

###### Art. 276.

With regard to crimes incriminated and sanctioned in Arts. 257 to 261 inclusive, 264 and 265, conditional liberty may not be granted.

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 233

##### CAPITULO VI

Del prevaricato

###### Art. 277.- The following are prevaricators and shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment:

1o.- Judges of law or arbitrators juris who, by personal interest, by affection or disaffection toward any person or corporation, or in prejudice of the public cause, or of an individual, render judgment against express Law, or proceed criminally against anyone, knowing that he does not deserve it;

2o.- Judges or arbitrators who give advice to one of the parties litigating before them, with prejudice to the opposing party;

3o.- Judges or arbitrators who in the processing of cases proceed maliciously against express laws, doing what they prohibit or failing to do what they mandate;

4o.- Public employees of any class who, exercising any judicial, governmental or administrative authority, by personal interest, affection or disaffection toward any person or corporation, deny, refuse or delay the administration of justice, or the protection or other remedy that is legally requested of them or that the public cause requires, provided they are obliged to do so; or who, when required or warned in legal form, by some legitimate authority or legitimate interested party, refuse or delay providing the cooperation or assistance that depends on their powers, for the administration of justice, or any necessity of the public service;

5o.- Other employees, officials and court clerks who, for any of the causes mentioned in the first numeral, fraudulently abuse their functions, prejudicing the public cause or any person; and, 6o.- Judges or arbitrators who hear cases in which they represented one of the parties as lawyers or attorneys.

Concordancias:

CODIGO TRIBUTARIO, Arts. 278

LEY DE LA JURISDICCION CONTENCIOSO ADMINISTRATIVA, Arts. 64

Jurisprudencia:

Gaceta Judicial, JUICIO POR PREVARICATO DE MAGISTRADOS, 01-jul-1993

Gaceta Judicial, COMPETENCIA POLICIAL O CIVIL DEL DELITO DE PREVARICATO, 18-ago-1993 Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE PREVARICATO, 31-oct-2001

###### Art. 278.- If the infractions detailed in the preceding article have been committed in

criminal matters, the maximum penalty shall be applied.

###### Art. 279.- Attorneys, defenders or legal representatives in court who disclose their client's secrets to the opposing party; or who, after having undertaken to defend one party and learning of their claims and means of defense, abandon them and defend the other; or who, in any other way, fraudulently, harm their client to favor the opponent or obtain some personal benefit, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.

Cross-references:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 1737

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 38

LAW OF THE BAR ASSOCIATION OF ECUADOR, Arts. 23, 49

###### Art. 280.- Court clerks who, in cases in which they participate, defend or advise any of the litigants, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year, a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America and loss of employment.

Note: Article amended by Art. 64 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 281.

Those who maliciously exercise the functions of a judge, verbally or in writing, in a civil or criminal case in which they have an interest, or in which a relative of theirs has an interest to the degree prohibited by law; or in which they have any other legal impediment to exercise such functions, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year.

Cross-references:

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 856

###### Art. 282.- Any public official who, without legal order from the competent superior, discloses or reveals any secret entrusted to them by reason of their position, or exhibits any document that should be kept confidential, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment.

Cross-references:

LABOR CODE, Arts. 546

LAW OF COMPANIES, Arts. 442

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 151, 154, 158

SECURITIES MARKET LAW, Arts. 214, 222

ORGANIC LAW OF INSTITUTIONS OF THE FINANCIAL SYSTEM, Arts. 88, 90, 92, 93, 94

###### Art. 283.- Attorneys who facilitate the actions of unauthorized practitioners by authorizing with their signature the writings of such persons shall be punished with imprisonment of two months to one year and suspension from professional practice for the same period.

Cross-references:

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 213

###### Art. 284.- Physicians, surgeons, pharmacists, midwives, or any other professional who is a depositary of a secret by reason of the profession they exercise and who reveals it even when testifying in court, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to six months and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America and the execution of the sentence may not be suspended. The case in which the law requires them to disclose such secret is excepted.

Note: Article amended by Art. 65 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

##### CAPITULO VII

On bribery

###### Art. 285.- Any public official and any person entrusted with a public service who accepts an offer or promise, or receives gifts or presents, to execute an act of their employment or office, even if just, but not subject to remuneration, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America, in addition to restitution of double what they have received. They shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America in addition to restitution of triple what was received, if they have accepted offers or promises, or received gifts or presents either to execute in the exercise of their employment or office a manifestly unjust act; or to refrain from executing an act of their duty.

Note: Article amended by Art. 66 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 131, 233

ORGANIC LAW OF INSTITUTIONS OF THE FINANCIAL SYSTEM, Arts. 189

Case law:

Judicial Gazette, BRIBERY OF SOCCER MATCH REFEREE, 03-mar-2004

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF BRIBERY, 30-jun-2004

###### Art. 286.

Any public official and any person entrusted with a public service who, by accepted offers or promises, by received gifts or presents, have executed, in the exercise of their position, an unjust act, or have refrained from executing an act that was within the scope of their duties, shall be punished with three to six years of minor confinement and with a fine of sixteen to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America, in addition to triple what they have received.

Note: Article amended by Art. 67 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 287.- The guilty party shall be sentenced to major confinement of four to eight years and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America, if they have accepted offers or promises, or received gifts or presents to commit, in the exercise of their position, a crime.

Note: Article amended by Art. 68 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 288.- The judge, arbitrator or mediator, the juror who has allowed themselves to be bribed or corrupted shall be punished with four to eight years of major confinement and deprivation of the practice of law, as applicable.

###### Art. 289.- The judge, arbitrator or juror guilty of bribery shall be sentenced, in addition to the penalties mentioned above, to a fine equal to triple the money or value of the reward. In no case may this fine be less than eight dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 69 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

LAW ON NARCOTIC AND PSYCHOTROPIC SUBSTANCES, Arts. 77

###### Art. 290.- Those who have compelled by violence or threats, corrupted by promises, offers, gifts or presents, a public official, a juror, arbitrator or mediator, or a person entrusted with a public service, to obtain an act of their employment or office, even if just, but not subject to remuneration, or the omission of an act within the scope of their duties, shall be punished with the same penalties as the official, juror, arbitrator or mediator guilty of having allowed themselves to be bribed.

Cross-references:

LAW ON NARCOTIC AND PSYCHOTROPIC SUBSTANCES, Arts. 77, 78

###### Art. 291.

In no case shall the things delivered by the briber, or their value, be returned to them; and they shall be confiscated and placed at the disposal of the President of the Republic, so that they may be allocated to public assistance establishments that they deem appropriate.

##### CAPITULO VIII

On crimes against judicial activity

###### Art. 292.- Any official or any Police agent who, having received notice of the commission of a crime, does not immediately bring it to the attention of an investigating judge, shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to six months.

Cross-references:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK III), Arts. 1011

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 26, 209

###### Art. 293.

Any physician, surgeon, dentist, midwife, or any other person who, in the exercise of a health profession, upon providing professional services, discovers a fact that presents the characteristics of a crime and does not report it to the police or to an investigating judge, shall be punished with a fine of eight to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America, unless the report could result in criminal liability for the person assisted.

Note: Article amended by Art. 70 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 294.- Anyone who, by means of accusation or denunciation, or anonymously, or with a

false name, asserts that a crime has been committed that has not existed, or who simulates the traces of an infraction, to seek a criminal prosecution tending to obtain a certification in their favor, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year. Concordances:

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 46, 51, 61

###### Art. 295.- Anyone who within a trial before the investigating judge or the trial judge, or

extrajudicially, before judicial authorities or police agents, declares themselves the author of a crime that has not been committed, or of a crime in which they have had no participation, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to two years.

The aforementioned crime shall not be punished when it has been committed to favor an ascendant or descendant, spouse or sibling, or a relative by affinity within the second degree.

###### Art. 296.- Anyone who in the course of civil or administrative proceedings, or

before or during criminal proceedings, in order to mislead the judge, artificially changes the state of things, places or persons, and if the act does not constitute another infraction punished more severely by this Code, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 71 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

##### CAPITULO VIII

Of Illicit Enrichment

Note: Chapter incorporated by Art. 2 of Law No. 6, published in Official Register Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

Art. ...- Illicit enrichment constitutes the unjustified increase in a person's assets, produced on the occasion of or as a consequence of the performance of a public office or function, which is not the result of their legally received income. Note: Article incorporated by Art. 2 of Law No. 6, published in Official Register Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

Note: Article substituted by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 131, 233

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ILLICIT ENRICHMENT, 18-Apr-2007

Art. ... .- Illicit enrichment shall be punished with a penalty of two to five years of imprisonment and the restitution of double the amount of the illicit enrichment, provided it does not constitute another crime.

Note: Article incorporated by Art. 2 of Law No. 6, published in Official Register Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

Note: Article substituted by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

###### Art. 296-C.- The two preceding unnumbered articles are applicable to those who, as

officials or employees, manage funds of the Central Bank, the Development and Commercial Credit System Banks, and the Ecuadorian Social Security Institute. Note: Article incorporated by Art. 2 of Law No. 6, published in Official Register Supplement 260 of August 29, 1985.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 131, 231, 233

##### CAPITULO IX

Of the publication and distribution of anonymous writings or without imprint

###### Art. 297.- Anyone who has contributed to the publication or distribution of

any printed, mimeographed or machine-written or handwritten materials, which do not bear the known name of the author or printer, or of the printing house, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year and a fine of twelve to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 72 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 146, 384

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, ANONYMOUS PRINTED MATERIALS, 17-Jan-1934

###### Art. 298.- The following shall be exempt from the penalty indicated in the previous article:

Those who have disclosed the printer, the author or the printing house; and, The sellers or distributors who have disclosed the persons from whom they received the printed, mimeographed, or written material.

###### Art. 299.- Once discovered, the printing house or mimeograph in which the anonymous publication

was made shall be confiscated and assigned to a public establishment.

Concordances:

INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY LAW, Arts. 324, 325

##### CAPITULO X

Of the crimes of suppliers

###### Art. 300.- Persons charged with supplying provisions for the Armed

Forces who, voluntarily and maliciously, have failed in the service to which they are obligated, shall be punished with minor confinement from three to six years if the infraction was committed during a campaign; and if in peacetime, with imprisonment from one to five years.

The same penalties shall apply, as the case may be, to the agents of the suppliers, if these agents have caused the service to fail deliberately and with malice.

###### Art. 301.- Public officials or agents commissioned or paid by the

Government, who have provoked or aided the guilty parties to cause the service to fail in time of war, shall be punished with minor confinement from six to nine years; and, in peacetime, with imprisonment from one to five years.

###### Art. 302.- When the cessation of service results from negligence on the part of the

suppliers, their agents, public officials, or agents commissioned or paid by the Government, the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to two years in time of war; and, otherwise, with a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 73 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 303.- Even if the service has not failed, if deliveries have been voluntarily delayed,

the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years in case of war; and with a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America, if the Republic is at peace.

Note: Article amended by Art. 74 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 304.

In the cases provided for by Art. 302, proceedings may not be pursued except by denunciation of the Minister of State to whom the matter concerns.

###### Art. 305.- If there has been fraud regarding the nature, quality or quantity of the things

supplied, the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

###### Art. 306.- Public officials, or agents or commissioners of the Government who

have participated in this fraud shall be punished with imprisonment from two to five years.

##### CAPITULO XI

Of escape

###### Art. 307.- In case of escape of detainees or prisoners, those in charge of transporting or

guarding them shall be punished in accordance with the following articles. Concordances:

LAW ON NARCOTIC AND PSYCHOTROPIC SUBSTANCES, Arts. 76 Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ESCAPE, 26-May-1942

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ESCAPE OF DETAINEE BY COMPLICITY, 06-Feb-2001

###### Art. 308.- If the fugitive is being pursued, or has been convicted of a crime that

merits imprisonment, said persons in charge shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months, in case of negligence, and with imprisonment from six months to two years, in case of connivance.

Concordances:

CODE OF EXECUTION OF PENALTIES AND SOCIAL REHABILITATION, Arts. 55

###### Art. 309.- If the fugitive is being pursued, or has been convicted of a crime that

merits confinement, said persons in charge shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year, in case of negligence; and with three years of minor confinement, in case of connivance.

###### Art. 310.- Those who, not being charged with guarding or transporting the detained or imprisoned person, have procured or facilitated the escape, shall be punished, in the case of Art. 308, with imprisonment of fifteen days to six months; and in the case of Art. 309, with imprisonment of three months to one year.

Excepted from this provision are the ascendants, descendants, spouses and siblings of the fugitive, and their relatives by affinity in the same degrees.

Concordances:

CODIGO CIVIL (TITULO PRELIMINAR), Arts. 22, 23

###### Art. 311.- If the escape has taken place or has been attempted with violence, threats, or prison break, the penalties against those who have facilitated it, by supplying instruments suitable for carrying it out, shall be:

In the circumstances stated in Art. 308, imprisonment of one to five years against those charged with guarding or transporting the fugitive, and three months to one year against other persons; and in the circumstances stated in Art. 309, minor imprisonment for four years against those charged with the fugitive, and imprisonment of six months to two years against other persons.

###### Art. 312.- If the escape has taken place or has been attempted with violence, threats, or prison break, the penalties against those who have facilitated it with weapons, shall be: In the circumstances stated in Art. 308, minor imprisonment of three to six years against those charged with the fugitive, and imprisonment of one to five years against other persons.

In the circumstances stated in Art. 309, major imprisonment of four years against those charged, and imprisonment of three years against other persons.

##### CAPITULO XII

Of prohibited games and raffles

###### Art. 313.- Those who establish houses or tables of prohibited games, without permission from the respective authority, shall be punished with imprisonment of three to six months and a fine of sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

The guilty may, in addition, be placed under special surveillance by the authority for at least six months and at most one year.

In all cases, the funds and effects that have been found exposed to the game shall be confiscated, as well as the furniture, instruments, utensils and apparatus intended for the service of the games.

Note: Article amended by Art. 75 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO IV), Arts. 2164, 2165, 2168

###### Art. 314.- Those who, in the gaming houses under their charge, allow minors, employees of stores or other commercial or industrial establishments, domestic servants or notoriously vagrant individuals, shall be punished with imprisonment of four months to one year and a fine of sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 76 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 315.- The promoters, operators, administrators, commissioners or agents of raffles not authorized by the Police shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America. Movable objects placed in the raffle and those used or intended for its service shall be confiscated.

When real estate has been placed in a raffle, confiscation shall not apply, which shall be replaced by a fine of sixty-two to one hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 77 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 316.- Those shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America, or with only one of these penalties:

Those who have placed, announced, or distributed tickets for raffles not permitted by the Police, which may only permit those determined by Law; and, Those who by notices, advertisements, posters, or by any other means of publication have made known the existence of these raffles.

In all cases, the tickets, as well as the notices, advertisements or posters shall be rendered unusable.
Note: Article amended by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Registry 621 of July 4, 1978.

Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Registry 36 of October 1, 1979, which orders a return to the previous legal text.
Note: Article amended by Art. 78 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 317.- Town criers and poster affixers who have made known the person from whom they received the tickets or the above-mentioned writings shall be exempt from the penalties indicated in the preceding article.

#### TITULO IV

OF CRIMES AGAINST PUBLIC FAITH

##### CAPITULO I

Of the counterfeiting of coins, bank notes, bearer securities and credit documents

###### Art. 318.- Those who counterfeit gold or silver coins that have legal tender in the Republic, or those who introduce, issue or put such coins into circulation, shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

The penalty shall be minor imprisonment of three to six years and a fine of sixteen to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America, if it concerns gold or silver coins that do not have legal tender in the Republic.

Note: Article amended by Art. 79 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 303

CODIGO DE COMERCIO, Arts. 573

Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, INFRACCION PENAL. ELEMENTOS ESENCIALES, 26-feb-1951

Gaceta Judicial, ESTAFA CON BILLETES FALSOS, 03-jul-1996

###### Art. 319.- If the crime mentioned in the previous article is committed with coins of other metal, which have legal circulation in the Republic, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of eight to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America. The penalty shall be three months to one year of imprisonment and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America, when dealing with coins of other metal, which do not have legal tender in the Republic.

Note: Article amended by Art. 80 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 320.- Those in charge of minting coins who exceed the quantity for which they were authorized, shall be punished as counterfeiters, in accordance with the distinctions established in the previous articles.

###### Art. 321.

Whoever with clipping, drilling, filing, or in any other manner alters the value of gold or silver coins that have legal circulation in the Republic, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of eight to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

When dealing with coins of other metal, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen to ninety days and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 81 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 322.- The alterations mentioned in the previous article, of gold or silver coins that do not have legal circulation in the Republic, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of eight to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America; and if it concerns coins of other metal, the penalty shall be imprisonment of eight to sixty days and a fine of six to nine dollars of the United States of North America.
Note: Article amended by Art. 82 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 323.

Those who become guilty of fraud in the election of the standards designated, according to the Monetary Law, for the verification of the law and weight of gold and silver coins, shall be punished with extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years.

###### Art. 324.- Those who have committed such fraud in the election of standards for

coins of other metal, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment.

###### Art. 325.- Anyone who, having received counterfeit or altered coins as good, has

put them back into circulation after having recognized or had their defects recognized, shall be punished with a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America and imprisonment of one to six months, or with only one of these penalties. Note: Article amended by Art. 83 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 326.- Those who imitate or counterfeit checks, bank notes whose issuance is authorized by

Law; titles of debts of the Treasury, Provincial Councils and Municipal Councils or any other Institution legally authorized to issue them; titles, certificates or bearer shares legally issued by Banks or Companies authorized to do so and the corresponding interest coupons, as well as those guilty of fraud in the issuance of bills and titles or interest coupons referred to in this article, shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years and a fine of nine to eighty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 84 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 2355

GENERAL LAW ON CHECKS, Arts. 9

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, COMPLICITY IN CASHING FORGED CHECK, 09-Dec-1993

###### Art. 327.

Those who, having procured bank notes by any means, put them into circulation without such circulation being legally authorized, shall be punished as counterfeiters.

###### Art. 328.- Those who issue bills, bills of exchange, or tokens as conventional currency,

or who, in the same manner, use any other object destining it for circulation, shall be punished with a penalty of eight days to six months of imprisonment and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 85 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

##### CHAPTER II

Of the counterfeiting of seals, stamps and marks

###### Art. 329.- Shall be punished with extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years:

Those who imitate or counterfeit national seals or stamps, adhesive or fixed, of whatever kind or value they may be; and, Those who, fraudulently, offer for sale or circulate these imitated or false stamps.

If the imitation or counterfeiting has been done in foreign territory, the penalty shall be six to nine years of minor imprisonment.

###### Art. 330.

Those who imitate or counterfeit punches, dies, plates, sheets or any other objects used for the manufacture of stamps, shares, bonds, coupons and bank notes whose issuance has been authorized by Law, shall be punished with the penalties and in accordance with the distinctions established in the preceding article.

###### Art. 331.

Anyone who, fraudulently, makes use of imitated or false seals or stamps, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 86 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF FALSIFICATION AND USE OF FALSE DOCUMENT, 16-Dec-2002

###### Art. 332.- Shall be punished with three months to one year of imprisonment:

Anyone who has counterfeited tickets for the transport of persons or things, or fraudulently made use of a false ticket; and, Anyone who has counterfeited the seal, stamp or mark of any authority, of a private establishment, of a bank, of industry, of commerce or of a private individual, or has fraudulently made use of these false seals, marks or stamps.

###### Art. 333.- Anyone who, having procured from any official or from a private individual the true punches,

plates, sheets or any other tools used for the manufacture of the objects expressed in Art. 330, has made an application or use thereof detrimental to the rights or interests of the State, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of six to nine years.

###### Art. 334.- Those who have imitated or counterfeited the official seals or stamps of

foreign nations shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment.

###### Art. 335.

Those who, fraudulently, make use of these foreign seals and stamps, imitated or false, shall suffer the penalty of six months to one year of imprisonment.

###### Art. 336.- Shall be punished with a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States

of North America:

Those who have caused to disappear from a postage stamp or other adhesive stamp, the mark indicating that it has already been used; and, Those who have fraudulently made use of a stamp from which said mark has been caused to disappear.

Note: Article amended by Art. 87 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

##### CHAPTER III

Of the falsification of documents in general

###### Art. 337.- Public officials who, in the exercise of their functions, have committed a falsification

consisting of, shall be punished with extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years:

In false signatures;

In alteration of minutes, deeds or signatures;

In impersonation;

In writings made or inserted in registers or other public documents, in writings or other judicial proceedings, after their formation or closure. Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 1716, 1717

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 164, 165, 180

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF FALSITY, 20-Jan-2004

###### Art. 338.- The public official who, when drafting documents corresponding to his position, has

distorted their substance or their details, shall be punished with the same penalty:

Whether by writing stipulations different from those agreed upon or dictated by the parties; Or by establishing as true, facts that were not.

###### Art. 339.- Any other person who has committed a falsification in public instruments, in commercial

or bank writings, agricultural or industrial pledge contracts or special commercial pledge, in writings or in any other judicial proceeding, shall be punished with a penalty of six to nine years of minor imprisonment:

Whether by false signatures;

Or by imitation or alteration of handwriting or signatures;

Or by having invented conventions, provisions, obligations or releases, or by having inserted them out of time in the documents;

Or by addition or alteration of the clauses, declarations or facts that those documents were intended to receive or verify.

Concordances:

COMPANIES LAW, Arts. 126

ORGANIC LAW OF INSTITUTIONS OF THE FINANCIAL SYSTEM, Arts. 80

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, FALSE DOCUMENT, 11-May-2009

###### Art. 340.

Anyone who, by any of the means indicated in the preceding article, commits falsification in private instruments, with the exception of checks, shall be punished with two to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 191, 193

COMPANIES LAW, Arts. 126

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, FALSITY OF PRIVATE INSTRUMENTS, 05-Mar-2004

###### Art. 341.

In the cases expressed in the preceding articles, whoever has fraudulently used the false document shall be punished as if they were the author of the falsification. Cross-references:

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 180

Case law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIMINAL OFFENSE, 26-feb-1951

Judicial Gazette, FRAUDULENT USE OF FALSIFIED DOCUMENTS, 02-abr-2007

Judicial Gazette, FRAUDULENT USE OF FALSE PRIVATE DOCUMENT, 19-mar-2009

###### Art. 342.- Whoever counterfeits bank notes whose issuance is not authorized shall be

punished with three to six years of lesser imprisonment.

###### Art. 343.- Whoever has imitated or falsified a passport, or any other public travel document,

national or foreign, or has fraudulently used the aforementioned documents, shall be punished with the penalty of ordinary lesser imprisonment of three to six years. Whoever has been induced to use such documents when they are a victim of illegal trafficking of migrants shall not have any liability.

Note: Article replaced by Law No. 91, published in Official Registry 716 of December 2, 2002.

###### Art. 344.- The public employee who has delivered a passport to a person they did not know,

without having had their name or status attested to by two individuals known to them, and in cases where the Law requires these formalities, shall be punished with a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

If the public employee had knowledge of the false assumption of name or status when they delivered the passport, they shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years. They shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years if they have acted motivated by gifts or promises.

Note: Article amended by Art. 88 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 345.- Whoever, in order to exempt themselves or free another from a legally owed

service, or from any other obligation imposed by Law, has forged a certificate of illness or incapacity, whether with the name of a doctor, surgeon or practitioner, or with any name, falsely adding any of these qualifications, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year.

###### Art. 346.- The doctor, surgeon or practitioner who, in order to favor someone, has falsely

certified illnesses or incapacities suitable for dispensing from a legally owed service, or from any other obligation imposed by Law, or to demand or claim a right, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

If they have been motivated by gifts or promises, they shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years, in addition to the aforementioned fine.

Note: Article amended by Art. 89 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 347.- Whoever has forged, with the name of a public official, a certificate attesting to

good conduct, indigence or any other circumstance suitable for attracting the benevolence of public authority or private individuals toward the person designated in said certificate, or to procure employment, credits or assistance for them, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year.

If the certificate has been forged with the name of a private individual, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two months.

###### Art. 348.- Those who have forged, with the name of a public official, any kind of

certificates that may compromise public or private interests, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years.

If the certificate has been forged with the name of a private individual, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of two months to one year.

Case law:

Judicial Gazette, USE OF FALSIFIED PUBLIC INSTRUMENT, 25-jun-2008

###### Art. 349.- Whoever has fraudulently used a false or forged certificate in the circumstances

enumerated in Arts. 344 through 348 inclusive, shall be punished with the penalties indicated by these articles and according to the distinctions they establish.

###### Art. 350.- The public official who, in the exercise of their functions, has delivered a false

certificate, or falsified a certificate, or fraudulently used a false certificate, shall be punished with three to six years of lesser imprisonment.

###### Art. 351.

Innkeepers or hotelkeepers who have fraudulently registered in their registry, with false or assumed names, the persons lodged in their establishment, or who have in any other manner falsified their registries, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of six to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article amended by Art. 90 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 352.- Employees or persons in charge of a telegraph office who have committed a

falsification in the exercise of their functions, inventing or falsifying telegraphic messages, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.

###### Art. 353.

Whoever has fraudulently used the false message shall be punished as if they were the author of the falsification.

Art. ...- Electronic falsification.- Guilty of electronic falsification are the person or persons who, with intent to profit or to cause harm to a third party, using any means, alter or modify data messages, or the information included therein, that is contained in any material support, information system or telematic system, whether by:

1.- Altering a data message in any of its elements or requirements of a formal or essential nature;

2.- Simulating a data message in whole or in part, in a manner that induces error regarding its authenticity;

3.- Assuming in an act the intervention of persons who have not had it or attributing to those who have intervened in the act, declarations or statements different from those they have made.

The crime of electronic falsification shall be sanctioned in accordance with the provisions of this Chapter.

Note: Article added by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.

##### CAPITULO IV

Of false testimony and perjury

###### Art. 354.- There is punishable false testimony when, in declaring, confessing, or reporting

before public authority, whether the informant is a private person or authority, one knowingly fails to tell the truth; and perjury, when it is done under oath.

Excepted are cases of confession and preliminary inquiry of the accused in criminal trials, and reports of authorities when they may incur criminal liability.

Cross-references:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 77

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 133, 214, 215, 230

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 117, 133, 137, 293

Case law:

Judicial Gazette, PERJURY, 20-may-1887

Judicial Gazette, PERJURY, 26-ago-1916

Judicial Gazette, PERJURY, 08-ene-1937

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF PERJURY, 03-dic-2002

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF PERJURY, 24-feb-2006

Judicial Gazette, PERJURY, 09-jul-2009

###### Art. 355.

False testimony shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years; and perjury, with lesser imprisonment of three to six years.

Case law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF PERJURY, 08-nov-2001

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF PERJURY, 26-nov-2001

###### Art. 356.- If false testimony or perjury is committed in a criminal case, to the detriment of

the accused, the penalty shall be eight to twelve years of major imprisonment.

###### Art. 357.- The same penalty shall be imposed whenever falsity or perjury is committed

for payment, or when the offender habitually offers to testify in court as a false witness. And such habit may be considered to exist if, an individual having testified falsely in two or more trials, their suspicious background, their lack of lawful and known trade or industry, or other circumstances, would make it reasonably supposable.

###### Art. 358.- If in the judgment it is declared that the falsity of the testimony is not established, but if the

lack of lawful occupation or other background that would make the conduct of the accused suspicious, the latter shall remain subject to the surveillance of the authority for two to five years.

###### Art. 359.

Those who bribe witnesses, experts or interpreters, or those who knowingly make use in court of false witnesses or experts, whether in their own case or that of their clients or represented parties, shall be punished as perpetrators of false testimony or perjury, as the case may be. The minimum penalty shall be increased by one year if the bribed witness, expert or interpreter were a peasant or montubio.

If it were a lawyer who incurs the offense determined in this article, in the same judgment they shall also be definitively deprived of professional practice. If a professional gives a report in which they fraudulently fail to tell the truth, they shall also be definitively deprived of professional practice, without prejudice to the other penalties established in this Chapter.

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO CIVIL, Arts. 250, 264

###### Art. 360.- Interpreters and experts shall be considered as witnesses for the purposes of the

preceding articles.

##### CAPITULO V

Of crimes relating to commerce, industries and auctions

###### Art. 361.- Whoever maliciously or fraudulently has communicated the secrets of the

factory in which they have been or are employed, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine of eight to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article amended by Art. 91 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordancias:

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 45, 310, 546

###### Art. 362.- Whoever, with the purpose of forcing the rise or fall of wages, or of attacking the free exercise of

industry or labor, has committed violence, uttered insults or threats, imposed fines, prohibition or any interdiction, whether against those who work, or against those who make others work, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America, or with one of these penalties only.

The same penalty shall be imposed on those who, by means of gatherings, near the establishments where work is done, or near the dwelling of those who direct the work, have attacked the freedom of masters or workers.

Note: Article amended by Art. 92 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 363.- The following shall be punished with imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of thirty-one to

one hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America:

1o.- Those who cause the price of goods, papers, effects or securities to rise or fall, by any fraudulent means, or by meeting or coalition among the principal holders of a commodity or kind, with the purpose of not selling it except at a determined price;

2o.- Those who offer public funds, or shares or obligations of a company or legal entity, affirming or suggesting false facts or circumstances; and, 3o.- The founder, administrator, director, manager or trustee of a company or of a legal entity of another kind, who publishes or authorizes a false or incomplete balance sheet or any other report, whatever the purpose may have been in verifying it. Note: Article amended by Art. 93 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordancias:

CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 233

LEY DE COMPAÑIAS, Arts. 126

LEY ORGANICA DE INSTITUCIONES DEL SISTEMA FINANCIERO, Arts. 125, 128, 208

LEY DE MERCADO DE VALORES, Arts. 52, 53

###### Art. 363-A.- Those who, without legal authorization, raise or participate, in any manner, in the rise of prices of basic

foodstuffs intended for human consumption, whether their action consists of monopoly, concealment, hoarding, speculation, displacement or any other fraudulent form that produces disappearance, price increase or limitation of both the production and marketing of such products, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and confiscation of the articles.

If it involves a legal entity, the responsibility or sanction shall fall upon its legal representatives, administrators or executives.

In case of recidivism, double the penalty and confiscation of the products or goods that are the subject of the offense shall be applied, the ownership of which shall pass to the National Company of Vital Products or to the Entity that replaces it, both for the present case and for that contemplated in the first paragraph of this article.

Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 87, published in Official Register 480 of January 25, 1974.

Concordancias:

CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 284, 335

###### Art. 363-B.- The Special Tribunals of the Republic, within their respective

jurisdictions, shall be competent to hear, process in accordance with the Law and sanction the crimes established in the preceding article.

Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 87, published in Official Register 480 of January 25, 1974.

###### Art. 363-C.- The Intendants and Police Commissioners and Agents of the Public Force,

shall proceed to the immediate capture of persons involved in the commission of the crimes specified in the first article of this Decree and, within twenty-four hours, shall place them at the orders of the corresponding Special Tribunal for their trial. Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 87, published in Official Register 480 of January 25, 1974.

Jurisprudencia:

Gaceta Judicial, FACULTADES DE LOS COMISARIOS DE POLICIA, 11-abr-1885

###### Art. 363-D.- Popular action is established to report the acts determined in the

###### first article of this Decree.

Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 87, published in Official Register 480 of January 25, 1974.

###### Art. 364.- The director, manager or administrator of a company or of a legal entity of another kind who

lends their assistance or consent to acts contrary to the statutes, laws or ordinances that govern them, as a result of which the legal entity or the company is rendered unable to satisfy its commitments, or in the need to be dissolved, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years. Concordancias:

LEY DE COMPAÑIAS, Arts. 17, 91

LEY ORGANICA DE INSTITUCIONES DEL SISTEMA FINANCIERO, Arts. 132

###### Art. 365.

The general commander, the chief of military zone, the chief of troops, the governor or political chief who, in the extent of the places where they have the right to exercise their authority, has employed the means indicated in number 1o of Art. 363, or has taken part in the speculation indicated therein, whether openly, whether by simulated acts or by interposition of persons, shall incur the penalties assigned to the offense, the minimum being increased by one year, and the fine having to be seventy-seven to three hundred eleven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 94 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 366.- Those who, by means of riots, or with violence or threats, have

disturbed public order in the markets, with the purpose of provoking looting, or solely with the purpose of forcing sellers to dispose of their merchandise at a price lower than that which would result from free competition, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to two years.

The leaders or promoters shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and placed under the surveillance of the authority for at least two years and at most five. Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, LOOTING, 28-oct-1912

Gaceta Judicial, ROBBERY AND LOOTING, 07-dic-1912

###### Art. 367.- Whoever has altered or deteriorated merchandise, or the materials or

instruments used for its manufacture, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

The imprisonment shall be six months to three years if the offense has been committed by a person employed in the factory, workshop or commercial establishment.

The penalty shall be one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty-one to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America, if the offense has been committed by employees of the factory, workshop or commercial establishment, with the purpose of discrediting the industry, or by bribery or corruption.

Note: Article amended by Art. 95 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY LAW, Arts. 319, 320, 321, 322, 323

CHAPTER ...

Of crimes relating to the illegal commercialization of fuels derived from hydrocarbons, including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels

Note: Chapter added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (1)...- Illegal trafficking of hydrocarbons, their derivatives, liquefied petroleum gas and

biofuels.- Natural persons or legal representatives of legal persons who, by fraudulent, deceitful or clandestine means, sell, offer, distribute or commercialize under any title in border areas, in seaports or river ports or in the territorial sea, for the purpose of illegally removing from the country any hydrocarbon, its derivatives including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels, shall be punished with a penalty of three to six years of ordinary minor imprisonment, a fine of one thousand to two thousand unified basic remunerations for workers in general, and special confiscation of the goods and means of transport that served for the commission of the crime.

The owner or administrator of supply plants, storage facilities, packaging facilities, distribution centers, commercialization companies, service stations, who are authorized for the storage, transport and commercialization of fuels, and who by their action or omission and in a fraudulent and deceitful manner, allow the perpetration of the crime indicated in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with a penalty of ordinary minor imprisonment of six to nine years, a fine of one thousand to two thousand unified basic remunerations for workers in general, and definitive closure of the establishment. Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (2)...- Storage, transportation and commercialization without due

authorization.- Those who commercialize, store, illegally transport hydrocarbon derivatives especially in border areas and territorial sea, without authorizations, remission guides and other permits required by law for the execution of such activities, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand unified basic remunerations for workers in general, and special confiscation of the goods used for the execution of the crime. The Director of DIGMER or his responsible delegates who illegally and fraudulently issue such authorizations shall be punished with equal penalties.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (3)...- Adulteration of hydrocarbon derivatives.- Whoever, in a fraudulent or deceitful manner to

obtain personal benefit or that of a third party, adulterates the quality or quantity of hydrocarbons, their derivatives including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels, shall be punished with imprisonment of two to three years and special confiscation of the goods used for the execution of the crime.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (4)...- Improper use of hydrocarbon derivatives.- Those who, for their own benefit or that of

third parties, use hydrocarbon derivatives, including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels, in activities other than those expressly permitted by law, shall be punished with imprisonment of one year and special confiscation of the goods used in the execution of the crime.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (5)...- Extraction of hydrocarbons, their derivatives, biofuels or mixtures that

contain them.- Whoever by fraudulent or clandestine means seizes hydrocarbons, their derivatives, including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels, when they are transported through an oil pipeline, gas pipeline, polyduct or through any other means, or when they are stored in immediate supply sources or pumping plants, shall be punished with a penalty of ordinary major imprisonment of six to nine years and a fine of two to three thousand unified basic remunerations for workers in general, and confiscation of the goods used in the execution of the crime.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (6)...- Damages to the State and environment.- Whoever fraudulently destroys the hydrocarbon

infrastructure and/or causes the destruction or deterioration of the environment shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of eight to twelve years, without prejudice to the perpetrator remedying the environmental damages and the recovery of goods to the State.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (7)...- Destination of goods subject to special confiscation.- Once the prosecutor

has determined the illicit origin of the hydrocarbons, their derivatives, liquefied petroleum gas or biofuels, he shall request the judge to deliver these to PETROCOMERCIAL so that it may give them the use convenient to the interests of the State. The judge, upon the prosecutor's request, shall order the immediate delivery to PETROCOMERCIAL of the goods used in the commission of the infraction.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (8)...- Sanctions for officials and members of the public force.- If the crimes

provided for in this Chapter are committed by officials, employees, public servants or members of the Public Force, who fraudulently and clandestinely or by their actions or omissions allow the commission of such crimes, the maximum of the penalties provided for each of them shall be applied.

Note: Article added by Law No. 85, published in Official Registry Supplement 170 of September 14, 2007.

###### Art. (9)...- Whoever paralyzes or suspends in an unjustified manner the public service

of sale or distribution of fuels, whether gasoline, diesel, liquefied gas and other petroleum-derived products or biofuels such as ethanol, shall be sanctioned with a fine of five hundred to one thousand unified basic remunerations for workers in general and the definitive revocation of the sale or operation permit granted by the National Directorate of Hydrocarbons, without prejudice to civil and criminal actions that may arise.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

Disposiciones Generales

PRIMERA.- Competencia.- The judges and criminal courts of the jurisdiction where the offense was committed or where the hydrocarbons, their derivatives, liquefied petroleum gas or biofuels or mixtures containing them were apprehended shall have jurisdiction to try the crimes provided for in this Law, as determined by Title One of the Code of Criminal Procedure.

Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

SEGUNDA.- The Public Prosecutor's Office, as holder of criminal action, shall take cognizance of cases, direct and promote the investigation of crimes classified and sanctioned in the preceding articles, for which it shall have the support of the Public Force, especially the National Police, who shall be subject to the regulations established in Chapter One of Book Four of the Code of Criminal Procedure; and, if grounds are found, shall accuse the alleged offenders, subject to the criminal procedure in force and to that contemplated in this Law and its Regulations.

Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

TERCERA.- The economic resources generated by the application of pecuniary sanctions and fines provided for in this Law shall enter a special account to be maintained by the National Directorate of Hydrocarbons, which shall be under the control and supervision of the Office of the Comptroller General of the State. Such resources shall be exclusively used for technology, monitoring, control and supervision established in the unnumbered article contained in article 6 of this Law.

Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

CUARTA.- Popular action is granted for the reporting of infractions established in this Law, in which case the informant shall be granted 25% of the fine imposed definitively on the perpetrators of the infractions. The report shall entail civil and criminal liabilities in the event that the denounced subject of control obtains a judicial declaration qualifying it as malicious or reckless. The respective regulations shall define the requirements for the exercise of popular action, which shall be issued within a maximum period of sixty days from the effective date of this Law. Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

QUINTA.- The National Directorate of Hydrocarbons shall provide and facilitate authorizations for the distribution of hydrocarbon derivatives and liquefied petroleum gas for rural or suburban areas that sell domestic quantities for local consumption, and that are intended for agricultural activities, small industry and artisanal activities. Failure to comply with the provisions of this General Provision shall be grounds for immediate removal or dismissal from their duties or immediate termination of the contractual relationship of those responsible, as appropriate.

The National Directorate of Hydrocarbons shall regulate matters relating to determining the scope and quantification of domestic quantities for local use specified in this General Provision.

Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .

SEXTA.- The National Directorate of Hydrocarbons shall prepare, in coordination with PETROCOMERCIAL or its successor, the monthly program of requirements for the marketing company for the supply of hydrocarbons and their derivatives, including liquefied petroleum gas and biofuels, for the entire national territory, which shall be approved by the Ministry in charge; for this purpose, population density, the automotive, industrial and shipping fleet, including artisanal fishing in each of the territorial jurisdictions shall be considered in order to guarantee timeliness, quality, quantity and price for the benefit of consumers and to prevent smuggling.

Those responsible for non-compliance with this provision shall be sanctioned with a fine of one hundred unified basic remunerations for workers in general and immediate dismissal from office. Semi-annual programming reports shall be sent to the National Congress.

Nota: Disposición agregada por Ley No. 85, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 170 de 14 de Septiembre del 2007 .
