Gaceta Judicial, IMPROPER CRIMINAL LEGAL CLASSIFICATION, 18-Nov-2009

###### Art. 451.- When two or more persons have participated in a robbery or other crime, all

shall be responsible for the murder committed on this occasion or in this context; unless it is proven who committed it, and that the others had no part in it, nor could remedy or prevent it.

Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, CO-AUTHORSHIP, 24-Jun-1977

Gaceta Judicial, CO-AUTHORSHIP, 07-Jun-1983

###### Art. 452.- Those who knowingly and willingly kill any ascendant or

descendant, spouse or sibling, shall be punished with special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Note: Article amended by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Register 621 of July 4, 1978 .

Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Register 36 of October 1, 1979 , which orders a return to the previous legal text. Note: Article replaced by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001 .

Cross-references:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK III), Arts. 1010

Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, CRIME OF UXORICIDE OF ONE'S SPOUSE, 17-Aug-2007

###### Art. 453.- The mother who kills her newborn child to conceal her dishonor, shall be

punished with the penalty of minor imprisonment of three to six years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on the maternal grandparents who, to conceal the dishonor of the mother, commit this crime.

Cross-references:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 66, 44, 45

###### Art. 454.- Whoever instigates or provides assistance to another to commit suicide, if the

suicide has been attempted or consummated, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to four years and a fine of eight to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 126 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002 .

Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, SUICIDE, 30-Jul-1903

###### Art. 455.- When wounds or blows, inflicted voluntarily, but without intent to kill, have

caused death, the offender shall be punished with three to six years of minor imprisonment.

They shall be punished with minor imprisonment of six to nine years, if they have committed these acts of violence with any of the circumstances detailed in Art. 450. Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, WOUNDS AND BLOWS THAT CAUSE DEATH WITHOUT INTENT, 30-Sep-1987

###### Art. 456.- If substances administered voluntarily, which can seriously impair health, have

been given without intent to cause death, but have produced it, the guilty party shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.

###### Art. 457.- In the offense mentioned in the preceding article, the intent to kill shall be

presumed if the person who administered the harmful substances is a physician, pharmacist or chemist; or if they possess knowledge in said professions, even if they do not have the titles or diplomas to practice them.

###### Art. 458.

In the cases mentioned in Arts. 454, 455 and 456, if the guilty party has committed the offense against their father or other ascendant, or descendant, spouse or sibling, the minimum of the penalties indicated in said articles shall be increased by two more years.

###### Art. 459.- Whoever has caused harm through lack of foresight or precaution, but without

intent to harm another, is guilty of unintentional homicide. Case law:

Gaceta Judicial, INTENTIONAL HOMICIDE, 18-Nov-1949

Gaceta Judicial, UNINTENTIONAL HOMICIDE, 16-Mar-1983

Gaceta Judicial, UNINTENTIONAL HOMICIDE DUE TO MEDICAL NEGLIGENCE, 05-Mar-2001

###### Art. 460.- Whoever unintentionally causes the death of another person, if the act

is not more severely punished, shall be penalized with imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article amended by Art. 127 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTAFA, 17-sep-1998

Gaceta Judicial, CAMBIO DE LA INFRACCION ACUSADA EN EL AUTO DE LLAMAMIENTO A PLENARIO, 17-mar-2004

###### Art. 461.- When in a fight or assault in which more than two persons take part, a death

results, without it being established who caused it, all those who exercised violence upon the person of the victim shall be deemed perpetrators, and the penalty of one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty-one to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America shall be applied.

Note: Article amended by Art. 128 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 462.- Homicide caused by an athlete, in the course of a sport and upon the

person of another athlete in play, shall not be punished when it clearly appears that there was no intent nor violation of the respective regulations, and provided that the sport is not prohibited in the Republic.

Otherwise, the general rules of this Chapter on homicide shall apply.
Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, DESAFIO DEL BOX, 22-jun-1936

##### CAPITULO II

Of injuries

###### Art. 463.- Whoever wounds or strikes another, causing an illness or incapacity

for personal work that exceeds three days but not eight, shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen days to three months and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

If any of the circumstances of Art. 450 concurs, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two to six months and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.
Note: Article amended by Art. 129 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 105

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, INCAPACIDAD PARA EL TRABAJO, 09-jun-1916

Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES Y HERIDAS, 01-jul-1948

Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 04-abr-1997

Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 16-feb-2009

Gaceta Judicial, GOLPES O HERIDAS, 17-feb-2009

Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 10-mar-2009

###### Art. 464.- If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or an incapacity for

personal work that exceeds eight days but does not exceed one month, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two months to one year and a fine of twelve to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

If any of the circumstances of Art. 450 concurs, the imprisonment shall be from six months to two years, and the fine from sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 130 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE HERIDAS, 07-sep-1953

Gaceta Judicial, JUICIO PENAL POR LESIONES, 19-oct-1953

Gaceta Judicial, HERIDAS, 03-oct-1960

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE GOLPES Y HERIDAS, 28-sep-1964

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO POR LESIONES, 12-ene-1995

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE LESIONES, 27-nov-1998

Gaceta Judicial, HERIDAS O LESIONES, 10-feb-2009

###### Art. 465.- If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or incapacity for

work that exceeds thirty days but does not exceed ninety, the penalties shall be imprisonment from six months to two years, and a fine of sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

In case any of the circumstances of Art. 450 concurs, the imprisonment shall be from one to three years, and the fine from sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 131 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

###### Art. 466.- If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or incapacity for

personal work that exceeds ninety days, or a permanent incapacity for the work to which the victim had habitually dedicated himself until then, or a serious illness, or the loss of a non-principal organ, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of sixteen to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

In case any of the circumstances of Art. 450 concurs, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two to five years and a fine of thirty-one to one hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 132 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 467.- The penalties shall be imprisonment from two to five years and a fine of thirty-one to one

hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America, if the blows or wounds have resulted in a certain or probably incurable illness, or a permanent incapacity for work, or a grave mutilation, or the loss or disablement of a principal organ.

The penalties shall be minor imprisonment from three to six years and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America, if any of the circumstances of Art. 450 concurs.

Note: Article amended by Art. 133 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Jurisprudence:

Gaceta Judicial, COARTADA, 31-ago-1976

Gaceta Judicial, COARTADA, 31-ene-1989

###### Art. 468.- Whoever causes another an illness or transitory incapacity for personal work

by voluntarily administering substances that may gravely alter health shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six months and a fine of twelve to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 134 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 469.- The penalty shall be imprisonment from two to five years, when said substances have

caused a certain or probably incurable illness, or a permanent incapacity for personal work, or the absolute loss or disablement of an organ.
Cross-references:

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 360, 361

###### Art. 470.- When in a fight or assault in which more than two persons take part,

wounds or injuries result, without it being established who caused them, all those who exercised violence upon the person of the victim shall be deemed perpetrators, and the penalty of fifteen days to one year of imprisonment and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America shall be applied.

Note: Article amended by Art. 135 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 471.- In the crimes mentioned in the preceding articles of this Chapter, if the

guilty party has committed the offense upon the person of the father or mother or another ascendant or descendant, upon the spouse or upon a sibling, the immediately higher penalty shall be applied.

###### Art. 472.- Whoever has caused unintentional wounds or injuries by lack of

foresight or precaution is guilty, and shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America, if the act is not more severely punished as a special crime.

Note: Article amended by Art. 136 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 473.

In the circumstances of Art. 462, when dealing with wounds or injuries, what is established therein shall apply.

Art. ...- Those who incite or are responsible for acts of violence within sporting venues or in their vicinity or other places that by their capacity can host massive public gatherings, before, during or after a sporting, artistic or other entertainment event and that cause injuries to third parties, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 63, published in Official Register 412 of December 7, 2006.

##### CAPITULO III

Of the abandonment of persons

###### Art. 474.- Those who have abandoned or caused to be abandoned a child in a non-solitary place; and those who have exposed or caused to be exposed the child, provided that it is not in a hospice or in a foundling home, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of six dollars of the

United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 137 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 45, 66

###### Art. 475.- The crimes provided for in the preceding article shall be punished with imprisonment of

six months to two years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America, if they have been committed by the parents, or by persons to whom the child was entrusted.

Note: Article amended by Art. 138 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 476.

If, as a consequence of the abandonment, the child is left mutilated or injured, the guilty parties shall be punished:

In the case provided for by Art. 474, with imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America; and, In that of Art. 475, with imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 139 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 477.

If the abandonment has caused the death of the child, the penalty shall be: In the case of Art. 474, imprisonment of one to three years; and in the case of Art. 475, imprisonment of five years.

###### Art. 478.- Those who have abandoned or caused to be abandoned a child in a solitary place shall be punished with imprisonment of

six months to three years.

###### Art. 479.- The imprisonment shall be from two to five years, if those guilty of abandonment in a

solitary place are the parents, or persons to whom the child was entrusted.

###### Art. 480.

If, as a consequence of the abandonment, the child is left injured or mutilated, the guilty party shall be punished with the maximum of the penalties set forth in the two preceding articles.

If the abandonment has caused death, in the case of Art. 478, the penalty shall be minor imprisonment of three to six years; and in the case of Art. 479, major imprisonment of four to eight years.

##### CAPITULO IV

Of the duel

Note: Chapter amended by Art. 140 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Note: Chapter repealed by Law No. 0, published in Official Register Supplement 555 of March 24, 2009.

##### CAPITULO V

Of the abuse of weapons

###### Art. 488.- Anyone who fires a firearm at a person, or attacks them with any other weapon, without wounding them,

provided that the act does not constitute an attempt, shall be punished with imprisonment of two years to five years.

Note: Article amended by Law No. 31, published in Official Register 231 of March 17, 2006.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK II), Arts. 973

#### TITULO VII

OF CRIMES AGAINST HONOR

##### CAPITULO UNICO

Of defamation

###### Art. 489.- Defamation is:

Slanderous, when it consists of the false imputation of a crime; and, Non-slanderous, when it consists of any other expression uttered in discredit, dishonor or contempt of another person, or in any action carried out with the same purpose. Concordances:

CONSTITUTION OF THE REPUBLIC OF ECUADOR, Arts. 66

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 2231

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 36

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, DEFAMATION, 23-Jul-1923

Judicial Gazette, SERIOUS NON-SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 11-Apr-1978

Judicial Gazette, SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 28-May-1982

Judicial Gazette, ELEMENTS OF THE CRIME OF DEFAMATION, 01-Sep-1999

Judicial Gazette, COMPENSATION FOR DEFAMATION, 03-Oct-2002

Judicial Gazette, DEFAMATION, 15-Nov-2007

Judicial Gazette, SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 08-May-2009

Judicial Gazette, CASSATION FOR DEFAMATION, 01-Jul-2009

Judicial Gazette, DEFAMATION, 14-Jul-2009

Judicial Gazette, FALSE IMPUTATION OF A CRIME, 23-Nov-2009

###### Art. 490.- Non-slanderous defamation may be serious or minor:

The following are serious:

1o.- The imputation of a vice or lack of morality whose consequences may considerably harm the reputation, credit, or interests of the aggrieved party;

2o.- Imputations that, by their nature, occasion or circumstance, are held in the public opinion to be insulting;

3o.- Imputations that reasonably merit the classification of serious, considering the status, dignity and circumstances of the offended and the offender; and, 4o.- Slaps, kicks, or other physical assaults.

Those that consist of attributing to another, facts, nicknames or physical or moral defects, that do not compromise the honor of the injured party, are minor.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 352

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, SERIOUS NON-SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 19-Jan-1999

Judicial Gazette, NON-SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 12-Feb-2009

###### Art. 491.- The offender of slanderous defamation shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two

years and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America, when the imputations have been made:

In public meetings or places;

In the presence of ten or more individuals;

By means of writings, printed or not, images or emblems fixed, distributed or sold, put up for sale, or exposed to public view; or, By means of unpublished writings, but addressed or communicated to other persons, including among these letters.

Note: Article amended by Art. 142 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK III), Arts. 1231, 1233

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 14-Nov-1966

Judicial Gazette, SLANDEROUS DEFAMATION, 06-Feb-2009

Judicial Gazette, CRIMINAL DEFAMATION, 23-Sep-2010

###### Art. 492.- Those who make the imputation privately, or in the presence of fewer than ten persons, shall be punished with one to six

months of imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 143 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 493.- Those who have directed to the authority imputations that constitute slanderous defamation shall be punished with one to three

years of imprisonment and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

If the imputations made to the authority constitute non-slanderous but serious defamation, the penalties shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to nineteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 144 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 494.- Those who have brought a judicial accusation, or made a complaint, that has not been proven during the trial, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to three

years and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article amended by Art. 145 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 46, 51, 61

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, RECKLESS OR SLANDEROUS PRIVATE PROSECUTION, 17-Apr-1998

###### Art. 495.- The perpetrator of grave non-slanderous insult, committed by word or deed, in writing, images or emblems, under any of the circumstances indicated in Art. 491, shall be punished with imprisonment of three to six months and a fine of six to twelve United States dollars; and under the circumstances of Art. 492, with imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and a fine of six United States dollars. Note: Article amended by Art. 146 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, PRESS TRIAL, 02-oct-1969

Judicial Gazette, CRIMES COMMITTED BY THE PRESS, 21-ene-1976

###### Art. 496.- When insults are reciprocal in the same act, none of the offended persons may initiate action for those that were inflicted in said act, whatever the gravity of the non-slanderous insults that were reciprocally directed; but there is no compensation between slanderous and non-slanderous insults. Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, COMPENSATION OF INSULTS INVALIDATES THE CRIME, 19-abr-2007

Judicial Gazette, ACQUITTAL OF DEFENDANTS, 27-mar-2009

###### Art. 497.- The accused of non-slanderous insult shall not be admitted proof of the truth of the imputations.

###### Art. 498.- Insults, slanderous or not, published in foreign media, may be prosecuted against persons who sent the articles or the order to insert them, or contributed to the introduction or distribution of such media in Ecuador.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF INSULT THROUGH ELECTRONIC MAIL, 07-may-2007

###### Art. 499.- Also responsible for insults, in any of their classes, are the reproducers of articles, images or insulting emblems, without being able to allege in this case, or in that of the previous article, as a cause of justification or excuse that said articles, images or emblems are nothing more than the reproduction of publications made in Ecuador or abroad.

###### Art. 499-A.- Defamation constitutes the disclosure, by any means of social communication or other public nature, except that authorized by Law, of the names and surnames of debtors either to require payment from them or using any form that indicates that the named person has that quality. Those responsible shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years. Note: Article added by Law No. 167, published in Official Registry 771 of June 22, 1984.

###### Art. 500.- Speeches made before judges or courts shall not give rise to action for insult, when the imputations were made in the exercise of defense of the cause; such as when objections are put to the adversary's witnesses and are proven, to invalidate the value of their testimony.

However, judges may, either ex officio or at the request of a party, order that writings containing insults of any kind be returned; warn the lawyers or the parties, and even impose a fine of up to sixteen United States dollars, applying for this purpose the rules of the Organic Law of the Judicial Function.

Imputations foreign to the cause give rise to the corresponding action, without prejudice to the fine referred to in the previous paragraph.

Note: Article amended by Art. 147 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 501.- Those guilty of any kind of insult who, outside the cases determined in the preceding articles, communicating with several persons, even in singular acts, with respect to each of them, offend the reputation, shall be punished as perpetrators of defamation, with a penalty of three months to one year of imprisonment and a fine of six to nineteen United States dollars; singular proof being admitted with respect to each of the acts, and provided that these exceed three.

Note: Article amended by Art. 148 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 502.- They do not commit insult: parents nor ascendants, with respect to their children and descendants; nor guardians, curators, employers, teachers, directors or heads of educational, correctional or punishment establishments, with respect to their wards, workers, students or dependents, unless the insult is one of those classified as slanderous.

#### TITLE VIII

OF PIMPING AND CORRUPTION OF MINORS

Note: Title of this Chapter substituted by Law No. 105, published in Official Registry

365 of July 21, 1998.

##### CHAPTER I

Of adultery

###### Art. 503.-Note: Article suppressed by the final article of the Code of Criminal Procedure,

No. 143, published in Official Registry 511 of June 10, 1983.

###### Art. 504.-Note: Article suppressed by the final article of the Code of Criminal Procedure,

No. 143, published in Official Registry 511 of June 10, 1983.

##### CHAPTER II

Of indecent assault, rape and statutory rape

Art. ..- Shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of four to eight years, whoever subjects a person under eighteen years of age or with disability, to force them to perform acts of a sexual nature, without carnal access. Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Registry 45 of June 23, 2005.

Note: Interpretation of the unnumbered Art. incorporated by article 9 of the Amending Law to the Criminal Code that Typifies the Crimes of Sexual Exploitation of Minors, published in Official Registry No. 45, of June 23, 2005, in the sense that: "The constitutive elements of the conducts that were typified until June 22, 2005, in articles 505, 506 and 507 of the Criminal Code, which sanctioned acts executed against the sexual integrity of persons under the age of majority, but without carnal access, considered as indecent assault, have not been eliminated, they are subsumed in the article being interpreted, since it is in force". The words "subjects"; and, "force them", contained in this article, shall be understood as momentary or permanent acts to bend the will of the victim and/or as the performance of actions with which it is intended to achieve or is achieved, through physical violence, threats or any form of inducement or deception directed to having a person under eighteen years of age or with disability, accept or obey and perform acts of a sexual nature, without carnal access, whether on the victim's own body, on the body of a third party or on the body of the perpetrator. Given by Law No. 53, published in Supplementary Official Registry 350 of September 6, 2006.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 13-abr-1916

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 07-may-1984

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 20-nov-1997

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 01-oct-2001

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 07-mar-2007

Judicial Gazette, INDECENT ASSAULT, 15-nov-2007

###### Art. 505.

Note: Article repealed by Law No. 2, published in Official Registry 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 506.

Note: Article repealed by Law No. 2, published in Official Registry 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 507.

Note: Article repealed by Law No. 2, published in Official Registry 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 508.- The attempted offense exists from the moment there is commencement of execution.

###### Art. 509.- Statutory rape is sexual intercourse with a person, using seduction or deceit

to obtain their consent.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 36

Jurisprudencia:

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTUPRO O VIOLACION, 28-sep-1959

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE VIOLACION O ESTUPRO, 31-may-1961

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTUPRO, 20-dic-1996

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTUPRO, 17-mar-1998

Gaceta Judicial, UNION DE HECHO CON UNA MENOR NO ES ESTUPRO, 28-jul-2000

Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTUPRO, 12-abr-2007

Gaceta Judicial, AUTOR DE DELITO DE ESTUPRO, 03-dic-2009

###### Art. 510.- Statutory rape shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to three years if the victim is

over fourteen years of age and under eighteen.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 36

###### Art. 511.

Nota: Artículo derogado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

###### Art. 511-A.

Whoever requests favors of a sexual nature, for themselves or for a third party, taking advantage of a situation of labor, teaching, religious or similar superiority, with the express or tacit announcement of causing the victim, or their family, harm related to the legitimate expectations they may have within the scope of said relationship, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever, in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, acts by taking advantage of the fact that they are in charge of proceedings or resolutions of any kind.

Whoever requests favors or makes malicious insinuations of a sexual nature that violate the sexual integrity of another person, and which is not provided for in the preceding paragraphs, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year. The sanctions provided for in this article necessarily include the permanent prohibition from engaging in activities that involve contact with the victim.

If sexual harassment is committed against minors, it shall be punished with imprisonment of two to four years.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de 21 de Julio de 1998 .

Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

###### Art. 512.- Rape is carnal access, with total or partial introduction of the male member,

by oral, anal or vaginal means; or, the introduction, by vaginal or anal means, of objects, fingers or organs other than the male member, to a person of any sex, in the following cases:

1o.- When the victim is under fourteen years of age;

2o.- When the offended person is deprived of reason or sense, or when due to illness or any other cause they cannot resist; and, 3o.- When violence, threat or intimidation is used.

Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de 21 de Julio de 1998 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

###### Art. 512-A.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de 21 de Julio de 1998 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 47, publicada en Registro Oficial 422 de 28 de Septiembre del 2001 .

Nota: Artículo derogado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

###### Art. 513.- The crime of rape shall be punished with special major imprisonment of sixteen

to twenty-five years, in number 1 of the preceding article; and, with extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years, in numbers 2 and 3 of the same article. Nota: Artículo sustituido por Decreto Supremo 2636, publicado en el Registro Oficial 621 de 4 de Julio de 1978 .

Nota: Decreto Supremo 2636 derogado por Decreto Legislativo s/n, publicado en el Registro Oficial 36 de 1 de octubre de 1979 , que ordena volver al texto legal anterior. Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 47, publicada en Registro Oficial 422 de 28 de Septiembre del 2001 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO I), Arts. 253

###### Art. 514.- If the rape produces a serious disturbance in the health of the

raped person, the penalty established for numerals 2 and 3 of the preceding article shall apply; and, if it causes their death, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

The same penalty of special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years, shall be imposed on those responsible for rape if the victims are their descendants, ascendants, siblings or relatives by affinity in direct line; and must in such case, be additionally sentenced to the loss of parental authority.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Decreto Supremo 2636, publicado en el Registro Oficial 621 de 4 de Julio de 1978 .

Nota: Decreto Supremo 2636 derogado por Decreto Legislativo s/n, publicado en el Registro Oficial 36 de 1 de octubre de 1979 , que ordena volver al texto legal anterior. Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 47, publicada en Registro Oficial 422 de 28 de Septiembre del 2001 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 2, publicada en Registro Oficial 45 de 23 de Junio del 2005 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO I), Arts. 283, 305

###### Art. 515.- The minimum of the penalties established by the preceding articles shall be increased

by four years:

If those responsible are among those who have authority over the victim.

If they are educators, or their servants, or servants of the persons designated above; If the assault has been committed either by public officials, or ministers of religion, who have abused their position to commit it; or by health professionals and personnel responsible for the care and attention of the patient, midwives, or practitioners, upon persons entrusted to their care; and, If in the cases of Arts. 507 and 512, the perpetrator, whoever they may be, has been assisted in the execution of the crime by one or more persons.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de 21 de Julio de 1998 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 47, publicada en Registro Oficial 422 de 28 de Septiembre del 2001 .

Jurisprudencia:

Gaceta Judicial, AGRAVAR LA PENA AL SINDICADO POR PARENTESCO CON AGRAVIADA, 27-ene-1994 Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE VIOLACION, 04-may-1998

###### Art. 516.- In cases of homosexuality, that do not constitute rape, the two

offenders shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years.

When homosexuality is committed by the father or other ancestor upon the person of the son or other descendant, the penalty shall be major imprisonment of eight to twelve years and deprivation of the rights and prerogatives that the Civil Code grants over the person and property of the child.

If it has been committed by ministers of worship, school teachers, college professors or tutors, upon persons entrusted to their direction or care, the penalty shall be major imprisonment of eight to twelve years.

Note: To partially accept the claim filed and declare the unconstitutionality of the first paragraph of article 516 of the Penal Code, and totally suspend the effects of said paragraph, which textually states: "In cases of homosexuality, that do not constitute rape, the two offenders shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years". Given by Constitutional Court Resolution No. 106, published in Official Registry Supplement 203 of November 27, 1997 .

###### Art. 517.- Bestiality shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years.

###### Art. 518.

Note: Article suppressed by Art. 20 of Supreme Decree No. 2636, published in Official Registry 621 of July 4, 1978 .

###### Art. 519.

Note: Article suppressed by Art. 20 of Supreme Decree No. 2636, published in Official Registry 621 of July 4, 1978 .

###### Art. 520.

Note: Article suppressed by Art. 20 of Supreme Decree No. 2636, published in Official Registry 621 of July 4, 1978 .

##### CHAPTER III

Note: Chapter III with its respective articles from 521 to 528 repealed by Law No.

105, published in Official Registry 365 of July 21, 1998 .

##### CHAPTER III

OF THE CRIMES OF PROCURING AND CORRUPTION OF MINORS

Art. ...- Whoever promotes or facilitates the prostitution of another person shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years, unless they have under their charge a house of tolerance, established in accordance with the regulations that the competent authority may issue for this class of establishments.

Note: Chapter and article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Art. ...- The penalty shall be six to nine years of extraordinary minor imprisonment, without the exemption of the previous article applying, when:

- 1. The victim is under fourteen years of age;
- 2. Violence, deceit, abuse of authority or any other coercive means is employed;
- 3. The victim is for any reason deprived of the capacity to give consent;
- 4. The perpetrator is a relative within the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity, or is a spouse, cohabitant, adopter, guardian or curator or has under their care for any reason the prostituted person;
- 5. The victim is in a situation of abandonment or extreme economic need; and,
- 6. The perpetrator has made procuring their profession or way of life.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Art. ...- A prison sentence of two to four years shall be imposed on whoever exploits the earnings obtained by a person who practices prostitution.

If the victim is under fourteen years of age, or a descendant, adopted child, child of their spouse or cohabitant or is under their care, the penalty shall be three to six years of ordinary minor imprisonment.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Art. ...- The conduct of one who through seduction or deceit abducts a person to deliver them to another with the purpose of having sexual relations shall be considered as procuring.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Art. ...- Whoever promotes or facilitates the entry or exit from the country or the transfer within the territory of the Republic of persons to practice prostitution shall be punished with a sentence of two to four years.

If any of the aggravating circumstances provided for in the previous articles is present, the penalty shall be three to six years of ordinary minor imprisonment.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

Art. ...- The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years:

- 1. The exposure, sale or delivery to minors under fourteen years of age of obscene objects, books, writings, visual or auditory images, that may seriously affect modesty or excite or pervert their sexual instinct; and,
- 2. Whoever incites a minor under fourteen years of age to drunkenness or the practice of obscene acts or facilitates their entry to brothels or other centers of corruption such as cinemas or theaters that provide obscene shows.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998 .

CHAPTER ...

OF THE CRIMES OF SEXUAL EXPLOITATION

Art. ..- Whoever produces, publishes or commercializes pornographic images, visual, audiovisual, computer, electronic materials or of any other physical support or format, or organizes live shows, with pornographic scenes in which persons over fourteen and under eighteen years of age participate, shall be punished with a penalty of six to nine years of ordinary minor imprisonment, the seizure of objects and property that are the product of the crime, and disqualification from employment, profession or occupation.

The same penalty shall be incurred by whoever distributes pornographic images, whose external characteristics make it manifest that in them is recorded or photographed the exhibition of persons over twelve and under eighteen years of age at the time of the creation of the image.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever facilitates access to pornographic shows or provides pornographic material in whose images minors participate. When in these infractions, the victim is a minor under twelve years of age or disabled, or a person who suffers from a serious incurable disease, the penalty shall be extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years, payment of compensation, seizure of objects and property that are the product of the crime, disqualification from employment, profession or occupation; and, in case of recidivism, the penalty shall be twenty-five years of special major imprisonment.

When the perpetrator of these crimes is the father, mother, relatives up to the fourth degree of consanguinity and second of affinity, guardians, legal representatives, curators or any person from the intimate circle of the family, ministers of worship, teachers and professors and any other person who by their profession or occupation has abused the victim, they shall be punished with a penalty of sixteen to twenty-five years of extraordinary major imprisonment, payment of compensation, seizure of objects and property that are the product of the crime, disqualification from employment, profession or occupation.

If the victim is under twelve years of age, the maximum penalty shall be applied.

Note: Chapter and Articles added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Whoever with violence, threat, intimidation or deceit uses persons of legal age in shows that imply the total or partial exhibition of their body for sexual purposes, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of four to eight years. Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Whoever organizes, offers or promotes tourist activities that imply services of a sexual nature, shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment of six to nine years and a fine of ten thousand to fifteen thousand dollars of the United States of America and the extinction of the legal entity or the closure of the business, if it belongs to a natural person.

The penalty shall be extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years, if the victim is a person under eighteen years of age and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America.

The penalty of extraordinary major imprisonment of nine to twelve years shall be applied, in the following cases:

- 1. When the victim is under twelve years of age;
- 2. When the offender takes advantage of the vulnerability of the victim or the victim is incapacitated to resist, or violence, threat or intimidation is used;
- 3. When the offender is a spouse, cohabitant or relative up to the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity of the victim; and,
- 4. If the offender has some type of relationship of trust or authority, or is a legal representative, stepfather or stepmother of the victim or minister of worship.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Whoever, by any means, acquires or contracts tourist activities, knowing that they imply services of a sexual nature with persons under eighteen years of age, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of eight to twelve years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Whoever promotes, induces, participates, facilitates or favors the recruitment, transportation, harboring, reception or delivery of persons resorting to threat, violence, deceit or any other fraudulent form for purposes of sexual exploitation, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of eight to twelve years. If the victim is a person under eighteen years of age, the maximum penalty shall be applied.

The penalty of extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years shall be applied, when one or more of the following circumstances concur:

- 1. If the victim is a person under twelve years of age;
- 2. If there is abuse of authority, or of a situation of need or vulnerability of the victim;
- 3. If the offender is a spouse, cohabitant or relative up to the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity of the victim;
- 4. If the offender has some type of relationship of trust, authority, is a legal representative, stepfather or stepmother of the victim or minister of worship; and,
- 5. If the victim, as a consequence of the crime, suffers a physical injury or permanent psychological damage or contracts a serious or fatal disease.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- In the event that by the commission of any of the crimes of this Chapter, the death of the victim occurs, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Whoever induces, promotes, favors, facilitates the sexual exploitation of persons under eighteen years of age, or of those who have disabilities, in exchange for remuneration or any other retribution, or appropriates all or part of these values, shall be punished with a penalty of ordinary minor imprisonment of six to nine years and the seizure of property acquired with the proceeds of the crime and payment of compensation for damages.

If the victim is under fourteen years of age, the penalty shall be extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years. In case of recidivism, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

CHAPTER ...

COMMON PROVISIONS TO SEXUAL CRIMES AND TRAFFICKING IN

PERSONS

Art. ..- In the case of concurrence of sexual crimes and/or trafficking in persons, the penalties shall accumulate up to a maximum of thirty-five years.

Note: Chapter and Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- In the crimes contemplated in Title VIII, of Book II of the Penal Code, the criminal action shall prescribe in double the time of the maximum penalty provided for each infraction, without the period being able to exceed fifty years. The penalty shall prescribe in a time equal to double the sentence, but the prescription period shall never be greater than thirty-five years nor less than five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- The public, non-private behavior of the victim, prior to the commission of the sexual crime or trafficking in persons, shall not be considered within the process. Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- In sexual crimes, consent given by the victim under eighteen years of age, shall be irrelevant. In crimes of trafficking in persons, consent shall be irrelevant.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- If the perpetrator or those responsible for the commission of sexual crimes or trafficking in persons, at the time of committing the infraction, exercises with respect to the victim their parental authority or legal representation, they shall be punished, in addition to the corresponding penalty, with the indefinite loss of these.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- When the media make an apology for sexual crimes and trafficking in persons, the respective legal representatives shall be punished with fines of up to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America, without prejudice to the seizure of the products or means used for their commission.

In case of recidivism, the closure and reversion of frequencies or authorization for their operation shall proceed.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005 .

Art. ..- Provisions related to conditional release, reduction of penalties, modification of the penalty, suspension of the execution of the penalty, conditional sentence and conditional release, provided for in Book I, Title IV, Chapter II of the Penal Code, shall not be considered or applied in the case of crimes contemplated in Title VIII of

## Book II of the Criminal Code, when they have been committed against persons

under eighteen years of age.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever alters the identity of a child; substitutes him for another; feigns a pregnancy or childbirth; delivers or records false or supposed data about a birth; usurps the legitimate paternity or maternity of a child; or falsely declares the death of a newborn, shall be punished with extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America.".

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

##### CHAPTER IV

Of abduction

###### Art. 529.- Whoever, for dishonest purposes, by means of violence, artifice or threats, has abducted

or caused to be abducted a minor over seven years of age, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 149 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, ABDUCTION, 03-mar-1888

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, 20-mar-1906

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, OF STATUTORY RAPE, OF SEDUCTION, 29-sep-1967

###### Art. 530.- If the abducted person is a girl under sixteen years of age, the penalty shall be

three to six years of minor imprisonment.

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, 23-jul-1982

###### Art. 531.- Whoever has abducted or caused to be abducted a woman over sixteen

years of age and under eighteen, who has consented to her abduction and voluntarily followed the abductor, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 36, 40

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIMES OF ABDUCTION AND STATUTORY RAPE, 02-jun-1994

###### Art. 532.

The abductor who marries the minor whom he has abducted or caused to be abducted, and those who have taken part in the abduction, may not be prosecuted except after the nullity of the marriage has been definitively declared. Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 96, 253

#### TITLE IX

OF CRIMES AGAINST CIVIL STATUS

##### CHAPTER I

Of the celebration of illegal marriages

###### Art. 533.- Whoever contracts a second or subsequent marriage, knowing that the

previous one was not lawfully dissolved, shall be punished with two to five years of imprisonment. Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 92, 105, 128

CODE OF PRIVATE INTERNATIONAL LAW SANCHEZ DE BUSTAMANTE, Arts. 52

###### Art. 534.- Whoever, in the celebration of marriages, does not comply with the laws

established in the Republic, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 81, 89, 100, 101, 102

###### Art. 535.- Whoever uses fraud or violence to evade the laws in force that regulate the

celebration or termination of marriage, shall be punished with three to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 105

###### Art. 536.- Whoever, in an illegal but valid marriage, causes the authority to intervene

by surprise or deceit, shall be punished with six months to two years of imprisonment; and, if he causes it to intervene with violence or intimidation, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.

If someone pretends to be an authority for the celebration of a marriage, he shall be punished with extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on the contracting party who has caused said pretended official to intervene.

###### Art. 537.- The guardian or curator who, before the legal approval of his accounts, contracts

marriage, or gives his consent for his children or descendants to contract it with the person whom he has or has had in guardianship, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 150 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 90

###### Art. 538.- The authority that celebrates a marriage for which there is a non-

dispensable impediment, shall be punished with a fine of eight to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America and imprisonment of one to five years.

If the impediment is dispensable, the penalty shall be reduced by half.

Note: Article amended by Art. 151 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 82, 89, 95, 96

###### Art. 539.- In all cases of this Chapter, the fraudulent contracting party shall be condemned to

endow, according to his ability, the woman who has contracted marriage in good faith.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 94

###### Art. 540.- The authority that issues dispensations and authorizations for the celebration of

a marriage, without prior presentation of the written consent of the parents, or curators of the minor contracting parties, or of the judge, as the case may be, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of sixty-two to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 152 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 82, 89

##### CHAPTER II

Of crimes aimed at destroying or preventing proof of the civil status of a child

###### Art. 541.- Whoever, having found a newborn child, has not delivered him within

three days to the political lieutenant or police authority of the place where he was found, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months.

###### Art. 542.- Those guilty of substitution of one child for another; or of feigning a birth; or of

usurpation of the civil status of a person, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 244, 261

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF KIDNAPPING OF A MINOR, 23-jul-1993

###### Art. 543.- Whoever has abducted or caused to be abducted a child, and provided that the crime

does not constitute kidnapping, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years, even if the child has voluntarily followed the guilty party.

Note: Including Errata, published in Official Register 165 of February 17, 1971, and published again in Official Register 173 of March 3, 1971.

###### Art. 544.- Whoever has maliciously concealed or caused to be concealed a child, if the act

is not more severely punished in this Code, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America. Note: Article amended by Art. 153 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 545.- Those who have taken or caused to be taken to a foundling home or other

establishments intended for that purpose, a child who was entrusted to them, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three months and a fine of six to nine dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 154 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK I), Arts. 269

###### Art. 546.- Those who, being in charge of a child, do not make it known to the persons who have

the right to claim him, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 155 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

GENERAL LAW OF CIVIL REGISTRY IDENTIFICATION AND IDENTITY CARDS, Arts. 62

#### TITLE X

OF CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY

##### CHAPTER I

Of theft

###### Art. 547.- Those who, without violence or threats against persons, nor

force against property, fraudulently take another's property, with intent to appropriate it, are guilty of theft.

Furthermore, individuals of recognized criminal conduct who habitually engage in the commission of crimes against property and who are registered as such in the State Security Offices are considered guilty of theft. The penalty for this class of offenders shall be one to three years of imprisonment.

Note: The Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal resolved to completely suspend due to substantive unconstitutionality the second paragraph of this article, the text of which precedes. Provision given by Resolution of the Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal, published in Official Register 632 of February 24, 1987.

Congress by Resolution published in Official Register 677 of May 4, 1987 ratifies the Resolution of the Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal. Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK II), Arts. 583, 584

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 1633

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 106

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, ISSUANCE OF CHECKS WITHOUT SUFFICIENT FUNDS, 22-Apr-1947

Judicial Gazette, CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY, 16-Feb-1948

Judicial Gazette, ISSUANCE OF CHECKS WITHOUT SUFFICIENT FUNDS, 06-Apr-1948

Judicial Gazette, ISSUANCE OF CHECKS WITHOUT SUFFICIENT FUNDS, 29-Aug-1949

Judicial Gazette, NOTIFICATION OF PROTESTED CHECK, 29-Aug-1949

Judicial Gazette, CONCURRENCE OF PUBLIC AND PRIVATE ACTION CRIMES, 21-Feb-1951 Judicial Gazette, CRIME AGAINST PROPERTY, 06-Jul-1953

Judicial Gazette, ILLEGITIMACY OF LEGAL STANDING, 27-Jan-1967

Judicial Gazette, MITIGATING CIRCUMSTANCES, 23-Jul-1996

Judicial Gazette, PRE-EXISTENCE OF PROPERTY FOR CRIME OF THEFT, 21-Dec-1998

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF THEFT OF CHECKS FROM SOLCA, 12-Jul-2001

###### Art. 548.- Theft shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to three years, taking into account

the value of the stolen property.

###### Art. 549.- The penalty shall be six months to five years of imprisonment:

1o.- When it involves machines or work instruments left in the field, or wires or other elements of fences, causing their total or partial destruction; 2o.- When the theft is committed on the occasion of a fire, explosion, flood, shipwreck, train accident, riot or mutiny, or taking advantage of the facilities arising from any other disaster or public disturbance;

3o.- When it involves tools, farming implements or other utensils, or animals that the victim needs for the exercise of his profession, art, trade or work; and, 4o.- When the persons from whom property is stolen are destitute or needy, or when what is stolen from them is sufficient to ruin their property.

##### CAPITULO II

Robbery

###### Art. 550.- Whoever, by means of violence or threats against persons or force against

property, fraudulently takes another's property, with intent to appropriate it, is guilty of robbery, whether the violence takes place before the act to facilitate it, at the moment of committing it, or after it has been committed to procure impunity.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 1633

CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 106

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, ROBBERY, 23-Sep-1933

Judicial Gazette, CRIMINAL LIABILITY, 16-Nov-1936

Judicial Gazette, ROBBERY OF MORTGAGE CERTIFICATES, 21-Jul-1938

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ROBBERY, 30-Sep-1939

Judicial Gazette, ELEMENTS OF ROBBERY, 02-Jul-1984

Judicial Gazette, ROBBERY, 26-Aug-2009

Judicial Gazette, PERPETRATOR OF CRIME OF ROBBERY, 25-Nov-2009

###### Art. 551.- Robbery shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and with ordinary minor confinement

of three to six years in cases where it is perpetrated with violence against persons, taking into consideration the value of the stolen property.

Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

###### Art. 552.- The maximum penalty established in the preceding article shall be applied to

the offender if any of the following circumstances concur:

1a.- If the violence has produced wounds that do not leave permanent injury;

2a.- If the robbery has been executed with weapons, or at night, or in an uninhabited place, or in a gang, or on roads or public thoroughfares;

3a.- If the robbery is perpetrated with perforation or breaking of a wall, fence, roof or floor, door or window of an inhabited place or its immediate dependencies; and, 4a.- When any of the circumstances of numbers 2o, 3o and 4o of Art. 549 concur.

When two or more of the circumstances referred to in this article concur, the penalty shall be ordinary minor confinement of six to nine years.

If the violence has caused a permanent injury of those detailed in Arts. 466 and 467, the penalty shall be ordinary major confinement of eight to twelve years.

If the violence has caused death, the penalty shall be special major confinement of sixteen to thirty years.

Note: Article amended by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Register 621 of July 4, 1978.

Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Register 36 of October 1, 1979, which orders return to the previous legal text. Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

Case Law:

Judicial Gazette, AGGRAVATING CIRCUMSTANCES, 18-Jan-1908

Judicial Gazette, ROBBERY WITH HOMICIDE, 12-Mar-1974

Judicial Gazette, EXISTENCE OF THE OFFENSE, 13-Jul-1977

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ROBBERY WITH DEATH, 08-Jul-1993

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF AGGRAVATED ROBBERY WITH DEATH, 13-Apr-1994

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF AGGRAVATED ROBBERY, 02-Apr-1996

Judicial Gazette, ELEMENTS OF THE CRIME OF INSULT, 01-Sep-1999

Judicial Gazette, ROBBERY IN A GANG, 27-Aug-2001

Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF AGGRAVATED ROBBERY, 01-Oct-2002

Art. ...- Those who, having dispossessed the driver or occupants of a motor vehicle, use it for the purpose of committing other crimes, even if there is no intent to appropriate the vehicle, shall be punished with ordinary minor confinement of three to six years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.

Art. ...- If as a consequence of the commission of these crimes serious physical injuries or psychological damage occur to the victim or victims, the perpetrator or perpetrators shall be punished with extraordinary major confinement of eight to twelve years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.

Art. ...- Those who in the commission of the crimes typified in this Law have caused the death or permanent incapacity of the victim or victims shall be punished with special major confinement of 16 to 25 years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.

###### Art. 553.- The taking of another's property committed with fraud and intent to

appropriate is assimilated to robbery, even if there are no acts of violence or threats against persons or force upon things, if it has been carried out in trains, streetcars, buses, docks, public gatherings or other agglomerations.

Also punishable with the penalty indicated in Art. 552, is the taking of objects, implements, materials or things belonging to installations intended for the service of the Fire Departments and the fraudulent purchase of such objects, materials or things. Case Law:

Gaceta Judicial, CRIME OF FRAUD, 21-ene-1947

Art. ...- Illicit appropriation.- Those who fraudulently use information systems or electronic networks to facilitate the appropriation of another's property, or those who procure the non-consensual transfer of property, securities or rights of a person, to the detriment of the latter or of a third party, for their own benefit or that of another person by altering, manipulating or modifying the operation of electronic networks, computer programs, computer systems, telematics or data messages, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand United States dollars.

Note: Article added by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.

Art. ...- The penalty of imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of one thousand to two thousand United States dollars, if the crime has been committed using the following means:

- 1. Disabling of alarm or security systems;
- 2. Discovery or decryption of secret or encrypted keys;
- 3. Use of magnetic or punched cards;
- 4. Use of remote opening controls or instruments; and,
- 5. Violation of electronic, computer or other similar security measures.

Note: Article added by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.

##### CAPITULO III

Cattle rustling

###### Art. 554.

The theft or robbery of horse, cattle, swine or sheep livestock, committed in places intended for their conservation, breeding or fattening, constitutes the crime of cattle rustling, without regard to the value of the stolen livestock.

Cross-references:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 106

Case Law:

Gaceta Judicial, CATTLE RUSTLING, 11-may-1921

Gaceta Judicial, CATTLE RUSTLING, 18-ene-1939

Gaceta Judicial, CATTLE RUSTLING, 22-feb-1960

###### Art. 555.- Cattle rustling shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years, in case

of theft; and from two to five years of imprisonment, in case of robbery. Recidivism shall be punished with double the said penalties.

###### Art. 556.- If cattle rustling committed with violence has caused wounds or injuries, or the

death of any person, the penalties established for such cases in the Chapter on robbery shall be applied to the guilty party.

##### CAPITULO IV

Extortion

###### Art. 557.- Whoever, by intimidation, or by simulating public authority, or a false order from

the same, compels another, without depriving him of personal liberty, to deliver, send, deposit, or place at his disposal or that of a third party things, money or documents that produce or may produce legal effects, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

###### Art. 558.- Whoever, by the same means, or with violence, compels another, without

depriving him of personal liberty, to sign or destroy documents of obligation or credit, shall incur the same penalty established in the preceding article.

###### Art. 559.- Whoever, by threatening imputations against honor, or violation of secrets, or

publications that affect honor or reputation, commits any of the acts expressed in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to four years.

##### CAPITULO V

Fraud and other fraudulent acts

###### Art. 560.

Whoever has fraudulently diverted or dissipated to the detriment of another, goods, money, merchandise, bills, receipts, writings of any kind, containing obligation or discharge, and which have been delivered to him with the condition of returning them, or making a specific use or employment of them, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of eight to sixteen United States dollars.

Note: Article amended by Art. 156 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Cross-references:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO I), Arts. 574

LEY DE COMPAÑIAS, Arts. 17

###### Art. 561.- Whoever has abused the needs, weaknesses or passions of a minor, to make

him sign, to his detriment, obligations, receipts, discharges, drafts, or any other obligatory documents, whatever the form in which this transaction has been made or disguised, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine of six to twelve United States dollars.

Note: Article amended by Art. 157 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 562.- Whoever, after having produced in court any title, document or brief, has

fraudulently removed it, in any manner whatsoever, shall be punished with a fine of six to thirty-one United States dollars.

This penalty shall be applied summarily by the court or judge having jurisdiction over the case. Note: Article amended by Art. 158 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 563.- Whoever, with the purpose of appropriating a thing belonging to another, has

had delivered to themselves funds, movables, obligations, receipts, acquittances, either by making use of false names, or false qualities, or by employing fraudulent schemes to cause belief in the existence of false enterprises, of an imaginary power, or credit, to instill the hope or fear of an event, accident, or any other chimerical occurrence, or to otherwise abuse confidence or credulity, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from eight to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

Shall be sanctioned with the maximum of the penalty provided in the preceding paragraph and a fine from five hundred to one thousand dollars of the United States of North America, whoever commits the crime using electronic or telematic means.

The penalty shall be ordinary minor imprisonment from three to six years, if the fraud is committed in cases of illegal migrations.

Note: Article amended by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.

Note: Article amended by Art. 159 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Note: Last paragraph added by Law No. 91, published in Official Registry 716 of December 2, 2002.

Concordances:

CODE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE, Arts. 540, 1000

LAW OF COMPANIES, Arts. 30, 128

ORGANIC LAW OF FINANCIAL SYSTEM INSTITUTIONS, Arts. 121

SECURITIES MARKET LAW, Arts. 213, 214

Judicial Gazette, CHECK PROTESTED FOR CLOSED ACCOUNT, 07-Dec-1995

Judicial Gazette, CHECK DRAWN ON CLOSED ACCOUNT, 23-Nov-1998

###### Art. 563-A.- It corresponds exclusively to the Ecuadorian Housing Bank, to the

Mutualist Associations of Savings and Credit for Housing and to the Housing Cooperatives with legal personality, whose registration has been recorded in the National Housing Board, to receive funds from natural persons, as quotas or contributions for urbanization programs or construction of social interest housing.

Those who receive or order to receive quotas, contributions or advances, contravening the provisions of the preceding paragraph, shall incur in the crime of fraud, which shall be prosecuted ex officio, and shall be sanctioned with the penalty established in the previous article. Popular action is established to denounce the crime typified in the preceding paragraph. Note: Inorganic amendment given by Art. 2 of Supreme Decree No. 1206, published in Official Registry 419 of October 26, 1973.

###### Art. 564.- Shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and fine from six to forty-

seven dollars of the United States of North America, or with only one of these penalties, whoever has deceived the buyer:

Regarding the identity of the thing sold, fraudulently delivering a thing different from the determined object on which the contract was based; and, Regarding the nature or origin of the thing sold, delivering a thing similar in appearance to what was purchased or believed to be purchased.

Note: Article amended by Art. 160 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

CIVIL CODE (BOOK IV), Arts. 1732, 1740

###### Art. 565.- Shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and fine from six to twenty-

five dollars of the United States of North America those who, with fraudulent schemes, have deceived the buyer regarding the quantity of things sold. Note: Article amended by Art. 161 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 566.- Shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen days to three months and fine from six to

sixteen dollars of the United States of North America:

Those who, without being in the case of Art. 428, because there is no danger of altering the health of consumers, have falsified or had falsified beverages or foodstuffs;

Those who have sold or had sold, publicly or privately, said falsified articles; and, Those who by posters or notices, printed or not, or by any other mode of propaganda, have taught or revealed procedures for the falsification of the mentioned articles.

Note: Article amended by Art. 162 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 567.- Shall be punished with the same penalty and fine from sixteen to sixty-two

dollars of the United States of North America, the importers, commission agents, or receivers of falsified beverages or foodstuffs.

Note: Article amended by Art. 163 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 568.- In dealing with the infractions determined in the preceding articles and in

Arts. 428, 429 and 430, the judge shall order the publication of the sentence, by posters and by the press, at the cost of the convicted person; and shall close the factories, stores, warehouses and storage facilities where the falsified articles are kept or sold, until the issuance of the sentence, and shall order the confiscation to which there would be place.

###### Art. 569.- Shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment from three to six years and fine from

six to sixteen dollars of the United States of America, whoever hides, keeps custody of, guards, transports, sells or transfers the possession, in whole or in part, of the goods, things or livestock, product of robbery or theft, "or whose legal origin cannot be proved". Note: Article amended by Art. 164 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Note: Article replaced by Law No. 0, published in Official Registry Supplement 160 of March 29, 2010.

Note: The phrase in quotation marks declared unconstitutional by Resolution of the Constitutional Court No. 29, published in Official Registry Supplement 343 of December 17, 2010.

Note: Again declares the unconstitutionality on the merits of article 569 of the Penal Code, in the phrase "... whose legal origin cannot be proved"; for this reason, the

###### article 569

ibidem shall be read hereafter as: "Art. 569.- "Shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment from three to six years and fine from six to sixteen dollars of the United States of America, whoever hides, keeps custody of, guards, transports, sells or transfers the possession, in whole or in part, of the goods, things or livestock, product of robbery or theft". Given by Resolution of the Constitutional Court No. 33, published in Official Registry Supplement 407 of March 18, 2011.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, TRADE WITH A MINOR, 25-Jan-1995

###### Art. 570.- In case of seizure, if the debtor or any other has fraudulently destroyed

or disposed of any of the objects on which the attachment was made, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to two years.

Jurisprudence:

Judicial Gazette, ARBITRARY DISPOSITION BY THE DEPOSITARY, 19-Dec-1962

###### Art. 571.- Shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to two years and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America:

Those who having found a movable thing belonging to another, whose value exceeds one hundred sucres, or having obtained its possession by chance, have concealed it or delivered it to a third party, fraudulently; and, Those who having discovered a treasure, have appropriated it to the detriment of those who, according to Law, have right to the treasure.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 165 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 572.- In the case of the second paragraph of the preceding article, if the value of the object that has not been returned to its owner does not exceed one hundred sucres, the concealer or holder shall be punished with the penalty of contravention only.

###### Art. 573.- Shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of six to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America, whoever has fraudulently obtained funds, securities or receipts, by means of a draft drawn against a person who does not exist, or who was not their debtor, or who should not have been so at the time of maturity, or who had not authorized them to draw against them.

However, prosecution may not take place, or shall cease, if the draft has been paid, or if the drawer has paid the value themselves, upon the fraud being discovered. Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 166 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 574.- The debtor who improperly removes or permits the removal from the place where industrial or agricultural exploitation is carried out of the objects given as industrial or agricultural pledge, or who through their negligence causes the disappearance or deterioration thereof, exchanges them, abandons them or gives as guarantee as their own agricultural or industrial goods that do not belong to them, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of nine to four hundred thirty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

The same penalty shall be incurred by the debtor of a special commercial pledge or agricultural or industrial pledge who sells, donates or gives as pledge to another person the object constituted as pledge without the intervention of the creditor, or who does not comply within the term set by the judge with the exhibition or delivery for auction sale of the object given as pledge, changes it from the place of conservation specified in the contract causing damages to the other party, or willfully destroys, damages or impairs the integrity thereof without settling the credit.

The same sanction shall be imposed on the debtor who sells the fruits of the objects pledged in industrial or agricultural pledge or the objects themselves, without notifying the buyer of the existence of the pledge contract.

These proceedings shall be initiated by order of the civil judge, who shall remit all proceedings to the Criminal Judge for the corresponding prosecution of the criminal process. If until rendering the investigation the pledge is placed at the disposal of the judge, the criminal trial shall be terminated and it shall be returned to the Civil Judge with the background submitted, for the continuation of the auction.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 167 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO II), Arts. 696

CODIGO DE COMERCIO, Arts. 576, 592

###### Art. 575.- Whoever has purchased movable goods with reservation of ownership and enters into contracts of sale, exchange, lease or pledge over them, takes them out of the country or delivers them to other persons without having paid the full price, except in case of express and written authorization of the seller, shall be punished with imprisonment from two months to three years.

The same penalty shall be applied to the buyer who willfully causes the disappearance of things acquired with reservation of ownership, who deteriorates or destroys them, who alters the marks, numbers, signs or who by any means prevents their identification. In both cases, the provisions of the 4th paragraph of the preceding article shall apply. Those who fraudulently present and sign false declaration and documentation aimed at obtaining illicit exchange or monetary benefits, through over-invoicing or under-invoicing in foreign trade, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years, without prejudice to the sanctions established in other legal provisions. Those who willfully perform acts through which they obtain improper exchange or monetary benefits, whether through false declarations or otherwise, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to four years and a fine of not less than 50% nor more than 200% of the values improperly obtained.

Nota: Artículo reformado por el artículo 37 de la Ley de Regulación Económica y Control del Gasto Público, No. 122, publicado en Registro Oficial Suplemento 453 de 17 de Marzo de 1983 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Decreto Ley Emergencia No. 2, publicado en Registro Oficial Suplemento 930 de 7 de Mayo de 1992 , Art.
