Note: Article amended by Art. 177 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Note: Numeral 1.- amended by Law No. 0, published in Supplementary Official Registry 555 of March 24, 2009.

Note: First paragraph and numeral 1. substituted by Law No. 0, published in Supplementary Official Registry 160 of March 29, 2010.

##### CAPITULO V

OF ENVIRONMENTAL CONTRAVENTIONS

Note: Chapter added by Law No. 49, published in Official Registry 2 of January 25, 2000.

###### Art. 607-A.- Shall be punished with imprisonment of five to seven days, and a fine of forty-four to eighty-eight dollars of the United States of North America, anyone who:

- a) Contaminates the air through emissions exceeding the permitted limits of vehicle exhausts;
- b) Accumulates garbage on public roads, on land or in front of houses or buildings;
- c) Makes noise due to lack of a silencer on their vehicle or through amplification equipment at high volume that disturbs public tranquility; or,
- d) Throws waste or contaminating water, destroys vegetation in parks or green spaces, in cases where such acts do not constitute a crime.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 49, publicada en Registro Oficial 2 de 25 de Enero del 2000 .

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 178 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

Concordancias:

CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 14, 66

#### TITULO II

SPECIAL PROVISIONS

REGARDING CONTRAVENTIONS

##### CAPITULO UNICO

###### Art. 608.- In everything relating to the punishability, responsibility or prescription of

contraventions, which is not regulated in a special manner, the provisions of Book I of this Code shall be observed. The rules of Arts. 82 and 87, however, do not apply to contraventions.

###### Art. 609.

Those sentenced to imprisonment, in accordance with the provisions of this Book, shall serve the sentence in the jails of their respective parishes or cantons; but in the event that these are lacking, they shall serve it in the jail of the provincial capital.

###### Art. 610.- Minors under fourteen years of age and over seven, who are found

playing, smoking or wandering in the streets, squares or any other public place, shall be immediately referred by the police to the respective Juvenile Court.

###### Art. 611.- The damages caused by those over seven years of age and under

eighteen, shall be paid by the parents, guardians, employers or persons on whom the offenders depended, in accordance with the provisions of the Civil Code. Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO IV), Arts. 2219, 2220, 2221, 2222

###### Art. 612.- When the same action or omission constitutes two or more contraventions,

the greater penalty shall be applied.

Nota: Artículo derogado por Decreto Supremo No. 2636, publicado en Registro Oficial 621 de 4 de Julio de 1978 .

Nota: Decreto Supremo 2636 derogado por Decreto Legislativo s/n, publicado en el Registro Oficial 36 de 1 de octubre de 1979 , que ordena volver al texto legal anterior.

###### Art. 613.- Reiteration shall be an aggravating circumstance.

###### Art. 614.- In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty established for the

last contravention committed shall be applied.

###### Art. 615.- For the graduation of penalties, the police judge shall take into account the

aggravating or mitigating circumstances that accompany the act, as follows:

If there is one or more aggravating circumstances, the maximum;

If there is one or more mitigating circumstances and no aggravating circumstances, the minimum.

###### Art. 616.- In the duration of a police penalty, all the time that the

guilty party has been detained, by reason of the same, shall be counted.

###### Art. 617.

The police action prescribes in thirty days, and the penalty in ninety days, both terms counted from the day on which the infraction was committed, or from the date on which the condemnatory sentence became final, respectively.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Decreto Supremo 2636, publicado en el Registro Oficial 621 de 4 de Julio de 1978 .

Nota: Decreto Supremo 2636 derogado por Decreto Legislativo s/n, publicado en el Registro Oficial 36 de 1 de octubre de 1979 , que ordena volver al texto legal anterior.

###### Art. 618.- The penalty of special confiscation shall prescribe within the term established for the

principal penalty; and civil convictions, according to the rules of the Civil Code.

###### Art. 619.- In the event that prosecution for a contravention has already been initiated, the

prescription period shall begin to run from the last judicial proceeding.

###### Art. 620.- Prescription may be declared ex officio, or at the request of a party.

###### Art. 621.- The police is obligated to guarantee all kinds of civil or

religious associations; but it shall prevent and dissolve those whose purpose is to disturb public tranquility or perpetrate an infraction; which is presumed if the individuals who compose them are armed or causing a disturbance.

The police authorities shall issue appropriate measures applicable to the case.

###### Art. 622.- Whenever the Intendant or another police authority

becomes aware that an attempt is being made to commit, or that a crime or contravention is being perpetrated, they shall take adequate and timely measures to prevent the realization of the criminal act, or its continuation, even by using force; always subject to the corresponding provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure.

###### Art. 623.- Regarding the detention of the accused in a contravention, the

corresponding provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure shall be observed.

Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 164, 165, 390

###### Art. 624.- Using or carrying weapons of any kind is prohibited, without prior

permission legally granted by the competent authority.

In the event that the competent authorities for the prosecution of contraventions confiscate weapons of dubious origin, without a valid legal permit, they must prepare the corresponding record, and immediately send the seized weapon to the Logistics Directorate of the Joint Command of the Armed Forces, and if the seized weapon serves as evidence of the crime, it shall be placed at the disposal of the competent authority. Nota: Según el Artículo 20 de la Ley de Fabricación, Importación, Exportación, Comercialización y Tenencia de Armas, Municiones, Explosivos y Accesorios, del Decreto Supremo No. 3757, publicado en Registro Oficial No. 311 de 7 de Noviembre de 1980 , la autorización para portar armas corresponde al Jefe del IV Departamento del Estado Mayor del Comando Conjunto de las Fuerzas Armadas.

Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 31, publicada en Registro Oficial 231 de 17 de Marzo del 2006 .

Concordancias:

CODIGO CIVIL (LIBRO II), Arts. 638

CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 46

###### Art. 625.

To travel freely in case of war, or when the Executive requires it, the corresponding permission from the police authority, manifested in a passport, shall be indispensable.

###### Art. 626.

The police and, especially, the Criminal Investigation Service Office, are obligated to investigate and discover thefts and other infractions, as well as to ascertain the whereabouts of stolen or lost items. Stolen or lost items that are found in the possession of any person who does not possess them with any title of ownership, shall be secured through deposit until the owner claims them or they are auctioned in accordance with the law.

###### Art. 627.- Anyone found in any public place in a state of mental

alienation shall be apprehended by the police and, following examination by physicians, shall be admitted to a psychiatric hospital or an appropriate establishment.

###### Art. 628.- The police is obligated to attend with its agents to theaters, circuses and, in

general, to any house, establishment or place where a public spectacle is to be presented, for the preservation of order and compliance with the respective programs.

###### Art. 629.- Without written permission from the police, no public spectacle shall be held,

except for those regulated by municipal ordinances.

###### Art. 630.- Police penalties are independent of compensation for damages and

losses caused by the offender. Concordancias:

CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 391

###### Art. 631.- Police authorities must prevent entry to the courts of their

jurisdiction to persons who, when not acting in their own defense, intend to act as pettifoggers.

FINAL CHAPTER

###### Art. 632.-Note: Article deleted by the final article of the Code of Criminal Procedure,

No. 134, published in Official Register 511 of June 10, 1983.
